## File: `flutter/app/android/app/src/main/kotlin/dev/fumiproject/phone_robot_app/ArCoreManager.kt`
```kt
package dev.fumiproject.phone_robot_app

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.net.Uri
import android.opengl.EGL14
import android.opengl.EGLConfig
import android.opengl.EGLContext
import android.opengl.EGLDisplay
import android.opengl.EGLSurface
import android.opengl.GLES11Ext
import android.opengl.GLES20
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.util.Log
import android.view.Surface
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.google.ar.core.ArCoreApk
import com.google.ar.core.Config
import com.google.ar.core.Coordinates2d
import com.google.ar.core.Frame
import com.google.ar.core.Pose
import com.google.ar.core.RecordingConfig
import com.google.ar.core.RecordingStatus
import com.google.ar.core.Session
import com.google.ar.core.TrackingState
import com.google.ar.core.exceptions.NotYetAvailableException
import com.google.ar.core.exceptions.UnavailableException
import io.flutter.plugin.common.EventChannel
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import io.flutter.view.TextureRegistry
import java.io.File
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.nio.FloatBuffer
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.hypot

/**
 * ARCore のセッション管理と、Dart 側チャネルへの配信。
 *
 * チャネル契約（Dart: platform/pose_source.dart, depth_source.dart）:
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/arcore": startPoseTracking / stopPoseTracking
 *  - EventChannel "phone_robot/arcore/pose": {x, y, heading}（SPEC §1 の部屋座標）
 *  - EventChannel "phone_robot/arcore/tracking": "TRACKING" / "PAUSED" /
 *    "STOPPED" / "UNSUPPORTED" / "ERROR"
 *  - EventChannel "phone_robot/depth": {ranges: [21本, m]}. 計測なしは -1.0。
 *
 * 座標変換（SPEC §5.1）: セッション開始後、最初に TRACKING になった瞬間の
 * カメラポーズを部屋の原点・方位基準に固定し、以降は相対ポーズから
 *   x = relTx, y = -relTz, heading = atan2(-forwardZ, forwardX)
 * を計算する（ARCore は右手系・-Z がカメラ前方、部屋座標は y-up 前方）。
 *
 * 深度（SPEC §5.2）: Raw Depth（16bit）＋信頼度画像から、画面中央帯の
 * 21 方向（-30°〜+30°、3°刻み）について距離の中央値を取る。
 * 信頼度が低い画素・範囲外は使わない。サンプルが無い方向は -1.0（計測なし）。
 * Depth 非対応の環境では常に全方向 -1.0 を返す（フォールバック）。
 *
 * 描画無しでトラッキングを回すため、オフスクリーンの EGL pbuffer と
 * ダミーの SurfaceTexture を作り、専用スレッドで session.update() を回す。
 */
class ArCoreManager(
    private val activity: Activity,
    private val textureRegistry: TextureRegistry? = null
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ArCoreManager"
        private const val CAMERA_REQUEST_CODE = 0xA001
        private const val RAY_COUNT = 21
        private const val FRAME_INTERVAL_MS = 33L
        private const val POSE_INTERVAL_MS = 33L
        private const val PREVIEW_INTERVAL_MS = 100L
        private const val DEPTH_INTERVAL_MS = 80L
        private const val THERMAL_PREVIEW_INTERVAL_MS = 250L
        private const val THERMAL_DEPTH_INTERVAL_MS = 300L
        private const val FALLBACK_INTERVAL_MS = 1000L
        private const val PREVIEW_WIDTH = 640
        private const val PREVIEW_HEIGHT = 480
    }

    @Volatile private var session: Session? = null
    @Volatile private var running = false
    private var worker: HandlerThread? = null

    @Volatile private var depthSupported = false
    private var originPose: Pose? = null
    private var lastTracking: String = ""
    private var lastDepthAt: Long = 0L
    private var lastPosePushAt: Long = 0L
    private var lastPreviewAt: Long = 0L
    private var lastCameraTimestamp: Long = -1L

    private var flutterTextureEntry: TextureRegistry.SurfaceTextureEntry? = null
    private var activeMp4Path: String? = null

    /** 前回配信した深度画像のタイムスタンプ（新しい深度かの判定用）。 */
    private var lastDepthTimestamp: Long = -1L

    private var poseSink: EventChannel.EventSink? = null
    private var trackingSink: EventChannel.EventSink? = null
    private var depthSink: EventChannel.EventSink? = null
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    private var pendingResult: MethodChannel.Result? = null

    val poseHandler = object : EventChannel.StreamHandler {
        override fun onListen(arguments: Any?, events: EventChannel.EventSink?) {
            poseSink = events
        }
        override fun onCancel(arguments: Any?) { poseSink = null }
    }

    val trackingHandler = object : EventChannel.StreamHandler {
        override fun onListen(arguments: Any?, events: EventChannel.EventSink?) {
            trackingSink = events
        }
        override fun onCancel(arguments: Any?) { trackingSink = null }
    }

    val depthHandler = object : EventChannel.StreamHandler {
        override fun onListen(arguments: Any?, events: EventChannel.EventSink?) {
            depthSink = events
        }
        override fun onCancel(arguments: Any?) { depthSink = null }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 開始
    fun start(result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        if (running) {
            result.success(null)
            return
        }
        if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(activity, Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
            != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        ) {
            pendingResult = result
            ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(
                activity, arrayOf(Manifest.permission.CAMERA), CAMERA_REQUEST_CODE
            )
            return
        }
        startSession(result)
    }

    fun onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode: Int, grantResults: IntArray) {
        if (requestCode != CAMERA_REQUEST_CODE) return
        val result = pendingResult ?: return
        pendingResult = null
        if (grantResults.isNotEmpty() && grantResults[0] == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            startSession(result)
        } else {
            result.error("camera_permission", "カメラ権限が許可されませんでした", null)
        }
    }

    fun getCameraTextureId(): Long {
        if (flutterTextureEntry == null && textureRegistry != null) {
            try {
                flutterTextureEntry = textureRegistry.createSurfaceTexture().also {
                    it.surfaceTexture().setDefaultBufferSize(PREVIEW_WIDTH, PREVIEW_HEIGHT)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "createSurfaceTexture failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
        return flutterTextureEntry?.id() ?: -1L
    }

    fun startMp4Recording(rawRunId: String): String? {
        val safeRunId = rawRunId.trim().replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]"), "")
        if (safeRunId.isEmpty()) return null
        val s = session ?: return null
        try {
            if (s.recordingStatus == RecordingStatus.OK) {
                s.stopRecording()
            }
            val runsRoot = activity.getExternalFilesDir("runs") ?: return null
            val runDir = File(runsRoot, "run_$safeRunId")
            if (!runDir.exists()) {
                runDir.mkdirs()
            }
            val mp4File = File(runDir, "camera_recording_$safeRunId.mp4")
            val recConfig = RecordingConfig(s)
                .setMp4DatasetUri(Uri.fromFile(mp4File))
                .setAutoStopOnPause(true)
            s.startRecording(recConfig)
            activeMp4Path = mp4File.absolutePath
            return activeMp4Path
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "startMp4Recording failed: ${e.message}")
            activeMp4Path = null
            return null
        }
    }

    fun stopMp4Recording(): String? {
        val path = activeMp4Path
        activeMp4Path = null
        val s = session
        if (s != null) {
            try {
                if (s.recordingStatus == RecordingStatus.OK) {
                    s.stopRecording()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "stopMp4Recording failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
        return if (path != null && File(path).exists()) path else null
    }

    private fun startSession(result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        try {
            val availability = ArCoreApk.getInstance().checkAvailability(activity)
            if (availability == ArCoreApk.Availability.UNSUPPORTED_DEVICE_NOT_CAPABLE) {
                pushTracking("UNSUPPORTED")
                result.error("arcore_unsupported", "この端末は ARCore に未対応です", null)
                return
            }
            val installStatus = ArCoreApk.getInstance().requestInstall(activity, true)
            if (installStatus == ArCoreApk.InstallStatus.INSTALL_REQUESTED) {
                // インストール画面へ遷移した。戻ってきたらもう一度開始してもらう。
                result.error(
                    "arcore_install",
                    "ARCore（Google Play 開発者サービス）の導入が必要です。完了後にもう一度開始してください",
                    null
                )
                return
            }
            getCameraTextureId()
            val s = Session(activity)
            val config = Config(s)
            depthSupported = s.isDepthModeSupported(Config.DepthMode.AUTOMATIC)
            config.depthMode =
                if (depthSupported) Config.DepthMode.AUTOMATIC else Config.DepthMode.DISABLED
            config.focusMode = Config.FocusMode.AUTO
            config.updateMode = Config.UpdateMode.LATEST_CAMERA_IMAGE
            s.configure(config)
            session = s
            originPose = null
            lastTracking = ""
            lastDepthAt = 0L
            lastPosePushAt = 0L
            lastPreviewAt = 0L
            lastCameraTimestamp = -1L
            lastDepthTimestamp = -1L
            running = true
            startWorker(s)
            result.success(null)
        } catch (e: UnavailableException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "ARCore unavailable: ${e.message}")
            pushTracking("UNSUPPORTED")
            result.error("arcore_unavailable", "ARCore を開始できません: ${e.message}", null)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "ARCore start failed: ${e.message}")
            pushTracking("ERROR")
            result.error("arcore_error", "ARCore 開始エラー: ${e.message}", null)
        }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 停止
    fun stop() {
        if (!running && session == null) return
        stopMp4Recording()
        running = false
        try {
            worker?.quitSafely()
            worker?.join(600)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "worker stop failed: ${e.message}")
        }
        worker = null
        try {
            session?.pause()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "pause failed: ${e.message}")
        }
        try {
            session?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "close failed: ${e.message}")
        }
        session = null
        originPose = null
        lastCameraTimestamp = -1L
        val entry = flutterTextureEntry
        flutterTextureEntry = null
        if (entry != null) {
            if (Looper.myLooper() == Looper.getMainLooper()) {
                try {
                    entry.release()
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                }
            } else {
                mainHandler.post {
                    try {
                        entry.release()
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        pushTracking("STOPPED")
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------- 更新ループ
    private fun startWorker(s: Session) {
        val thread = HandlerThread("arcore-update-loop")
        thread.start()
        worker = thread
        Handler(thread.looper).post { glLoop(s) }
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun currentDisplayRotation(): Int {
        return try {
            activity.windowManager.defaultDisplay?.rotation ?: Surface.ROTATION_0
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            Surface.ROTATION_0
        }
    }

    private fun displaySizeForRotation(rot: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
        return if (rot == Surface.ROTATION_0 || rot == Surface.ROTATION_180) {
            PREVIEW_HEIGHT to PREVIEW_WIDTH
        } else {
            PREVIEW_WIDTH to PREVIEW_HEIGHT
        }
    }

    /** 最小 EGL 環境で session.update() を 30Hz 上限で回し、ライブプレビュー用テクスチャも更新する。 */
    private fun glLoop(s: Session) {
        val display = EGL14.eglGetDisplay(EGL14.EGL_DEFAULT_DISPLAY)
        val version = IntArray(2)
        EGL14.eglInitialize(display, version, 0, version, 1)

        val configs = arrayOfNulls<EGLConfig>(1)
        val numConfigs = IntArray(1)
        val bothAttribs = intArrayOf(
            EGL14.EGL_RED_SIZE, 8,
            EGL14.EGL_GREEN_SIZE, 8,
            EGL14.EGL_BLUE_SIZE, 8,
            EGL14.EGL_ALPHA_SIZE, 8,
            EGL14.EGL_RENDERABLE_TYPE, EGL14.EGL_OPENGL_ES2_BIT,
            EGL14.EGL_SURFACE_TYPE, EGL14.EGL_PBUFFER_BIT or EGL14.EGL_WINDOW_BIT,
            EGL14.EGL_NONE
        )
        EGL14.eglChooseConfig(display, bothAttribs, 0, configs, 0, 1, numConfigs, 0)
        if (numConfigs[0] <= 0 || configs[0] == null) {
            val pbufferOnlyAttribs = intArrayOf(
                EGL14.EGL_RED_SIZE, 8,
                EGL14.EGL_GREEN_SIZE, 8,
                EGL14.EGL_BLUE_SIZE, 8,
                EGL14.EGL_ALPHA_SIZE, 8,
                EGL14.EGL_RENDERABLE_TYPE, EGL14.EGL_OPENGL_ES2_BIT,
                EGL14.EGL_SURFACE_TYPE, EGL14.EGL_PBUFFER_BIT,
                EGL14.EGL_NONE
            )
            EGL14.eglChooseConfig(display, pbufferOnlyAttribs, 0, configs, 0, 1, numConfigs, 0)
        }
        val chosenConfig = configs[0]
        if (chosenConfig == null) {
            Log.w(TAG, "No matching EGLConfig")
            pushTracking("ERROR")
            EGL14.eglTerminate(display)
            return
        }

        val contextAttribs = intArrayOf(EGL14.EGL_CONTEXT_CLIENT_VERSION, 2, EGL14.EGL_NONE)
        val context = EGL14.eglCreateContext(display, chosenConfig, EGL14.EGL_NO_CONTEXT, contextAttribs, 0)
        val surfaceAttribs = intArrayOf(EGL14.EGL_WIDTH, 1, EGL14.EGL_HEIGHT, 1, EGL14.EGL_NONE)
        val pbufferSurface = EGL14.eglCreatePbufferSurface(display, chosenConfig, surfaceAttribs, 0)
        EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(display, pbufferSurface, pbufferSurface, context)

        val textureIds = IntArray(1)
        GLES20.glGenTextures(1, textureIds, 0)
        GLES20.glBindTexture(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, textureIds[0])
        GLES20.glTexParameteri(
            GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES,
            GLES20.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,
            GLES20.GL_LINEAR
        )
        GLES20.glTexParameteri(
            GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES,
            GLES20.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,
            GLES20.GL_LINEAR
        )
        GLES20.glTexParameteri(
            GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES,
            GLES20.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,
            GLES20.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE
        )
        GLES20.glTexParameteri(
            GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES,
            GLES20.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,
            GLES20.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE
        )
        val surfaceTexture = SurfaceTexture(textureIds[0])

        val previewBlitter = PreviewBlitter(
            display = display,
            eglConfig = chosenConfig,
            context = context,
            pbufferSurface = pbufferSurface,
            cameraTextureId = textureIds[0],
            targetTexture = flutterTextureEntry?.surfaceTexture()
        )
        previewBlitter.init()

        var lastRotation = -1
        var lastRotationCheckAt = 0L

        try {
            lastRotation = currentDisplayRotation()
            val (dispW, dispH) = displaySizeForRotation(lastRotation)
            s.setDisplayGeometry(lastRotation, dispW, dispH)
            s.setCameraTextureName(textureIds[0])
            s.resume()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "session resume failed: ${e.message}")
            pushTracking("ERROR")
            running = false
        }

        while (running) {
            val loopStart = System.currentTimeMillis()
            try {
                if (loopStart - lastRotationCheckAt >= 500L) {
                    lastRotationCheckAt = loopStart
                    val rot = currentDisplayRotation()
                    if (rot != lastRotation) {
                        lastRotation = rot
                        val (dispW, dispH) = displaySizeForRotation(rot)
                        s.setDisplayGeometry(rot, dispW, dispH)
                    }
                }
                val frame = s.update()
                val camTs = frame.timestamp
                if (camTs == 0L || camTs == lastCameraTimestamp) {
                    // LATEST_CAMERA_IMAGE はノンブロッキングのため、未更新時は待機してバッテリー消耗を防ぐ
                    try {
                        Thread.sleep(8)
                    } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
                        break
                    }
                    continue
                }
                lastCameraTimestamp = camTs
                try {
                    surfaceTexture.updateTexImage()
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    // テクスチャ更新失敗は次フレームで回復する
                }
                val previewInterval =
                    if (isThermalThrottled(loopStart)) THERMAL_PREVIEW_INTERVAL_MS else PREVIEW_INTERVAL_MS
                if (loopStart - lastPreviewAt >= previewInterval) {
                    lastPreviewAt = loopStart
                    previewBlitter.drawFrame(frame)
                }
                onFrame(frame)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (!running) break
                Log.w(TAG, "update failed: ${e.message}")
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(50)
                } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
                    break
                }
            }
            val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - loopStart
            val remaining = FRAME_INTERVAL_MS - elapsed
            if (remaining > 2L && running) {
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(remaining.coerceAtMost(25L))
                } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
                    break
                }
            }
        }

        previewBlitter.release()
        surfaceTexture.release()
        EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(
            display, EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE, EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE, EGL14.EGL_NO_CONTEXT
        )
        EGL14.eglDestroySurface(display, pbufferSurface)
        EGL14.eglDestroyContext(display, context)
        EGL14.eglTerminate(display)
    }

    private fun onFrame(frame: Frame) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val camera = frame.camera
        val stateName = when (camera.trackingState) {
            TrackingState.TRACKING -> "TRACKING"
            TrackingState.PAUSED -> "PAUSED"
            TrackingState.STOPPED -> "STOPPED"
            else -> "UNKNOWN"
        }
        if (stateName != lastTracking) {
            lastTracking = stateName
            pushTracking(stateName)
        }
        if (camera.trackingState == TrackingState.TRACKING) {
            if (originPose == null) {
                // 最初の TRACKING フレームを部屋の原点・方位基準に固定（SPEC §5.1）
                originPose = camera.pose
            }
            val origin = originPose
            if (origin != null && now - lastPosePushAt >= POSE_INTERVAL_MS) {
                lastPosePushAt = now
                val (x, y, heading) = relativePose(origin, camera.pose)
                pushPose(x, y, heading)
            }
        }
        if (depthSupported && camera.trackingState == TrackingState.TRACKING) {
            val depthInterval =
                if (isThermalThrottled(now)) THERMAL_DEPTH_INTERVAL_MS else DEPTH_INTERVAL_MS
            if (now - lastDepthAt >= depthInterval) {
                lastDepthAt = now
                val rays = computeRays(frame)
                if (rays != null) {
                    pushDepth(rays)
                }
            }
        } else if (!depthSupported && now - lastDepthAt >= FALLBACK_INTERVAL_MS) {
            // Depth 非対応環境のフォールバック: 常に「計測なし」（SPEC §5.2）
            lastDepthAt = now
            pushDepth(DoubleArray(RAY_COUNT) { -1.0 })
        }
    }

    @Volatile private var lastThermalCheckAt = 0L
    @Volatile private var thermalThrottled = false
    private val powerManager: PowerManager? by lazy {
        activity.getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as? PowerManager
    }

    private fun isThermalThrottled(nowMs: Long): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) return false
        if (nowMs - lastThermalCheckAt >= 2000L) {
            lastThermalCheckAt = nowMs
            val status = powerManager?.currentThermalStatus ?: PowerManager.THERMAL_STATUS_NONE
            thermalThrottled = status >= PowerManager.THERMAL_STATUS_MODERATE
        }
        return thermalThrottled
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------- 座標変換
    private data class HorizBasis(
        val fwdX: Double,
        val fwdZ: Double,
        val rightX: Double,
        val rightZ: Double,
        val leftX: Double,
        val leftZ: Double
    )

    /** Pose から重力整合ワールド水平面 (X, Z) 上の前方・右・左単位ベクトルを求める。 */
    private fun horizontalBasis(pose: Pose): HorizBasis {
        val zAxis = pose.zAxis
        var fx = -zAxis[0].toDouble()
        var fz = -zAxis[2].toDouble()
        var len = hypot(fx, fz)
        if (len < 1e-3) {
            // 真下・真上近くを向いた場合はカメラ上辺方向 (+Y) の水平成分を使う
            val yAxis = pose.yAxis
            fx = yAxis[0].toDouble()
            fz = yAxis[2].toDouble()
            len = hypot(fx, fz)
        }
        if (len < 1e-4) {
            fx = 0.0
            fz = -1.0
        } else {
            fx /= len
            fz /= len
        }
        // 右手系ワールド (+Y が鉛直上): forward = (fx, fz) に対して right = (-fz, fx), left = (fz, -fx)
        return HorizBasis(
            fwdX = fx,
            fwdZ = fz,
            rightX = -fz,
            rightZ = fx,
            leftX = fz,
            leftZ = -fx
        )
    }

    /**
     * origin の水平方位を北 (+y / +π/2)、右向きを +x として、cam の位置と方位を
     * SPEC §1 の部屋座標へ射影する（縦持ち・横持ち・斜め傾けに不変）。
     */
    private fun relativePose(origin: Pose, cam: Pose): Triple<Double, Double, Double> {
        val base = horizontalBasis(origin)
        val cur = horizontalBasis(cam)
        val dX = (cam.tx() - origin.tx()).toDouble()
        val dZ = (cam.tz() - origin.tz()).toDouble()
        val x = dX * base.rightX + dZ * base.rightZ
        val y = dX * base.fwdX + dZ * base.fwdZ
        val fRoomX = cur.fwdX * base.rightX + cur.fwdZ * base.rightZ
        val fRoomY = cur.fwdX * base.fwdX + cur.fwdZ * base.fwdZ
        val heading = atan2(fRoomY, fRoomX)
        return Triple(x, y, heading)
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------- Depth → 21本レイ（3D 逆射影）
    /**
     * 深度画像の各画素を camera.pose を用いて重力整合ワールド空間へ 3D 逆射影し、
     * 床・天井を高さ・俯角フィルタで除外した上で水平 ±30° の 21 本レイへ集計する。
     * 新しい深度画像でないフレームでは null を返し、配信しない。
     */
    private fun computeRays(frame: Frame): DoubleArray? {
        val rays = DoubleArray(RAY_COUNT) { -1.0 }
        var depthImage: android.media.Image? = null
        var confidenceImage: android.media.Image? = null
        try {
            depthImage = frame.acquireRawDepthImage16Bits()
            if (depthImage.timestamp == lastDepthTimestamp) {
                return null // 前回配信したのと同じ深度: 二重更新を避ける
            }
            lastDepthTimestamp = depthImage.timestamp
            confidenceImage = frame.acquireRawDepthConfidenceImage()
            val w = depthImage.width
            val h = depthImage.height
            val depthPlane = depthImage.planes[0]
            val depthBuffer = depthPlane.buffer
            val depthRowStride = depthPlane.rowStride
            val depthPixelStride = depthPlane.pixelStride
            val confPlane = confidenceImage.planes[0]
            val confBuffer = confPlane.buffer
            val confRowStride = confPlane.rowStride
            val confPixelStride = confPlane.pixelStride

            val intr = frame.camera.textureIntrinsics
            val texW = intr.imageDimensions[0].toDouble()
            val texH = intr.imageDimensions[1].toDouble()
            if (texW <= 0.0 || texH <= 0.0) return null
            val fx = intr.focalLength[0].toDouble() * w / texW
            val fy = intr.focalLength[1].toDouble() * h / texH
            val cx = intr.principalPoint[0].toDouble() * w / texW
            val cy = intr.principalPoint[1].toDouble() * h / texH
            if (fx <= 0.0 || fy <= 0.0) return null

            val camPose = frame.camera.pose
            val xAxis = camPose.xAxis
            val yAxis = camPose.yAxis
            val zAxis = camPose.zAxis
            val ax0 = xAxis[0].toDouble()
            val ax1 = xAxis[1].toDouble()
            val ax2 = xAxis[2].toDouble()
            val ay0 = yAxis[0].toDouble()
            val ay1 = yAxis[1].toDouble()
            val ay2 = yAxis[2].toDouble()
            val az0 = zAxis[0].toDouble()
            val az1 = zAxis[1].toDouble()
            val az2 = zAxis[2].toDouble()

            val basis = horizontalBasis(camPose)
            val bins = Array(RAY_COUNT) { ArrayList<Double>() }

            var v = 0
            while (v < h) {
                var u = 0
                while (u < w) {
                    val dIndex = v * depthRowStride + u * depthPixelStride
                    if (dIndex + 1 < depthBuffer.limit()) {
                        val cIndex = v * confRowStride + u * confPixelStride
                        val confidence =
                            if (cIndex < confBuffer.limit()) confBuffer.get(cIndex).toInt() and 0xFF else 0
                        if (confidence >= 100) {
                            val lo = depthBuffer.get(dIndex).toInt() and 0xFF
                            val hi = depthBuffer.get(dIndex + 1).toInt() and 0xFF
                            val mm = lo or (hi shl 8)
                            if (mm in 150..5000) {
                                val depthM = mm / 1000.0
                                val xCam = (u - cx) * depthM / fx
                                val yCam = (cy - v) * depthM / fy
                                val zCam = -depthM

                                val wx = ax0 * xCam + ay0 * yCam + az0 * zCam
                                val wy = ax1 * xCam + ay1 * yCam + az1 * zCam
                                val wz = ax2 * xCam + ay2 * yCam + az2 * zCam

                                val dForward = wx * basis.fwdX + wz * basis.fwdZ
                                if (dForward > 0.12) {
                                    val dLeft = wx * basis.leftX + wz * basis.leftZ
                                    val horizDist = hypot(dForward, dLeft)
                                    if (horizDist >= 0.28 &&
                                        horizDist <= 5.0 &&
                                        wy >= -0.03 &&
                                        wy <= 0.50
                                    ) {
                                        val elevSlope = wy / horizDist
                                        if (elevSlope >= -0.02 && elevSlope <= 0.35) {
                                            val angleDeg = Math.toDegrees(atan2(dLeft, dForward))
                                            if (angleDeg >= -31.5 && angleDeg <= 31.5) {
                                                val bin = ((angleDeg + 31.5) / 3.0)
                                                    .toInt()
                                                    .coerceIn(0, RAY_COUNT - 1)
                                                bins[bin].add(horizDist)
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    u += 3
                }
                v += 3
            }

            for (i in 0 until RAY_COUNT) {
                val samples = bins[i]
                if (samples.size >= 2) {
                    samples.sort()
                    rays[i] = samples[samples.size / 2]
                }
            }
        } catch (e: NotYetAvailableException) {
            // このフレームにはまだ深度が無い: null を返して配信しない
            return null
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "depth compute failed: ${e.message}")
        } finally {
            try {
                depthImage?.close()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
            try {
                confidenceImage?.close()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
        }
        return rays
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------- 配信
    private fun pushPose(x: Double, y: Double, heading: Double) {
        mainHandler.post {
            poseSink?.success(mapOf("x" to x, "y" to y, "heading" to heading))
        }
    }

    private fun pushTracking(state: String) {
        mainHandler.post { trackingSink?.success(state) }
    }

    private fun pushDepth(rays: DoubleArray) {
        val list = rays.toList()
        mainHandler.post { depthSink?.success(mapOf("ranges" to list)) }
    }

    /** ARCore の外部 OES カメラ映像を Flutter Texture 用の Window Surface へ描画する。 */
    private inner class PreviewBlitter(
        private val display: EGLDisplay,
        private val eglConfig: EGLConfig,
        private val context: EGLContext,
        private val pbufferSurface: EGLSurface,
        private val cameraTextureId: Int,
        private val targetTexture: SurfaceTexture?
    ) {
        private var windowSurface: EGLSurface = EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE
        private var program = 0
        private var posLoc = -1
        private var texLoc = -1
        private var samplerLoc = -1

        private val quadCoordsBuffer: FloatBuffer = ByteBuffer
            .allocateDirect(8 * 4)
            .order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder())
            .asFloatBuffer()
            .apply {
                put(floatArrayOf(-1f, -1f, 1f, -1f, -1f, 1f, 1f, 1f))
                position(0)
            }

        private val quadTexBuffer: FloatBuffer = ByteBuffer
            .allocateDirect(8 * 4)
            .order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder())
            .asFloatBuffer()
            .apply {
                put(floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 1f, 1f))
                position(0)
            }

        fun init() {
            val st = targetTexture ?: return
            try {
                val attrs = intArrayOf(EGL14.EGL_NONE)
                windowSurface = EGL14.eglCreateWindowSurface(display, eglConfig, st, attrs, 0)
                if (windowSurface == EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE) return

                if (!EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(display, windowSurface, windowSurface, context)) {
                    release()
                    return
                }
                val vShader = compileShader(
                    GLES20.GL_VERTEX_SHADER,
                    """
                    attribute vec2 aPosition;
                    attribute vec2 aTexCoord;
                    varying vec2 vTexCoord;
                    void main() {
                      gl_Position = vec4(aPosition, 0.0, 1.0);
                      vTexCoord = aTexCoord;
                    }
                    """.trimIndent()
                )
                val fShader = compileShader(
                    GLES20.GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER,
                    """
                    #extension GL_OES_EGL_image_external : require
                    precision mediump float;
                    varying vec2 vTexCoord;
                    uniform samplerExternalOES sTexture;
                    void main() {
                      gl_FragColor = texture2D(sTexture, vTexCoord);
                    }
                    """.trimIndent()
                )
                if (vShader == 0 || fShader == 0) {
                    if (vShader != 0) GLES20.glDeleteShader(vShader)
                    if (fShader != 0) GLES20.glDeleteShader(fShader)
                    release()
                    return
                }
                val prog = GLES20.glCreateProgram()
                GLES20.glAttachShader(prog, vShader)
                GLES20.glAttachShader(prog, fShader)
                GLES20.glLinkProgram(prog)
                GLES20.glDeleteShader(vShader)
                GLES20.glDeleteShader(fShader)
                val linkStatus = IntArray(1)
                GLES20.glGetProgramiv(prog, GLES20.GL_LINK_STATUS, linkStatus, 0)
                if (linkStatus[0] == 0) {
                    GLES20.glDeleteProgram(prog)
                    release()
                    return
                }
                program = prog
                posLoc = GLES20.glGetAttribLocation(prog, "aPosition")
                texLoc = GLES20.glGetAttribLocation(prog, "aTexCoord")
                samplerLoc = GLES20.glGetUniformLocation(prog, "sTexture")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "PreviewBlitter init failed: ${e.message}")
                release()
            } finally {
                EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(display, pbufferSurface, pbufferSurface, context)
            }
        }

        fun drawFrame(frame: Frame) {
            if (windowSurface == EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE || program == 0) return
            try {
                quadCoordsBuffer.position(0)
                quadTexBuffer.position(0)
                frame.transformCoordinates2d(
                    Coordinates2d.OPENGL_NORMALIZED_DEVICE_COORDINATES,
                    quadCoordsBuffer,
                    Coordinates2d.TEXTURE_NORMALIZED,
                    quadTexBuffer
                )
                quadCoordsBuffer.position(0)
                quadTexBuffer.position(0)

                if (!EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(display, windowSurface, windowSurface, context)) {
                    return
                }
                GLES20.glViewport(0, 0, PREVIEW_WIDTH, PREVIEW_HEIGHT)
                GLES20.glClear(GLES20.GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT)
                GLES20.glUseProgram(program)

                GLES20.glActiveTexture(GLES20.GL_TEXTURE0)
                GLES20.glBindTexture(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, cameraTextureId)
                GLES20.glUniform1i(samplerLoc, 0)

                GLES20.glEnableVertexAttribArray(posLoc)
                GLES20.glVertexAttribPointer(posLoc, 2, GLES20.GL_FLOAT, false, 0, quadCoordsBuffer)
                GLES20.glEnableVertexAttribArray(texLoc)
                GLES20.glVertexAttribPointer(texLoc, 2, GLES20.GL_FLOAT, false, 0, quadTexBuffer)

                GLES20.glDrawArrays(GLES20.GL_TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4)

                GLES20.glDisableVertexAttribArray(posLoc)
                GLES20.glDisableVertexAttribArray(texLoc)
                EGL14.eglSwapBuffers(display, windowSurface)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "PreviewBlitter draw failed: ${e.message}")
            } finally {
                EGL14.eglMakeCurrent(display, pbufferSurface, pbufferSurface, context)
                GLES20.glViewport(0, 0, 1, 1)
            }
        }

        fun release() {
            if (program != 0) {
                try {
                    GLES20.glDeleteProgram(program)
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                }
                program = 0
            }
            if (windowSurface != EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE) {
                try {
                    EGL14.eglDestroySurface(display, windowSurface)
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                }
                windowSurface = EGL14.EGL_NO_SURFACE
            }
        }

        private fun compileShader(type: Int, src: String): Int {
            val shader = GLES20.glCreateShader(type)
            if (shader == 0) return 0
            GLES20.glShaderSource(shader, src)
            GLES20.glCompileShader(shader)
            val status = IntArray(1)
            GLES20.glGetShaderiv(shader, GLES20.GL_COMPILE_STATUS, status, 0)
            if (status[0] == 0) {
                GLES20.glDeleteShader(shader)
                return 0
            }
            return shader
        }
    }
}
```

```kt
package dev.fumiproject.phone_robot_app

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.ContentProvider
import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.database.Cursor
import android.database.MatrixCursor
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Environment
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor
import android.provider.MediaStore
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import android.util.Base64
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodCall
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileNotFoundException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

/**
 * externalFilesDir("runs") 配下の走行ログファイルを content:// URI として
 * Google ドライブや他アプリへ安全に渡す ContentProvider（androidx 依存なし）。
 */
class LogFileProvider : ContentProvider() {

    override fun onCreate(): Boolean = true

    override fun query(
        uri: Uri,
        projection: Array<out String>?,
        selection: String?,
        selectionArgs: Array<out String>?,
        sortOrder: String?
    ): Cursor {
        val file = resolveSafeFile(uri)
        val cols = projection ?: arrayOf(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, OpenableColumns.SIZE)
        val cursor = MatrixCursor(cols, 1)
        val row = Array<Any?>(cols.size) { i ->
            when (cols[i]) {
                OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME -> file?.name ?: uri.lastPathSegment ?: "file"
                OpenableColumns.SIZE -> file?.length() ?: 0L
                else -> null
            }
        }
        cursor.addRow(row)
        return cursor
    }

    override fun getType(uri: Uri): String {
        val name = (uri.lastPathSegment ?: "").lowercase()
        return LogStorageManager.mimeTypeFor(name)
    }

    override fun openFile(uri: Uri, mode: String): ParcelFileDescriptor {
        val file = resolveSafeFile(uri)
            ?: throw FileNotFoundException("走行記録ファイルが見つかりません: $uri")
        return ParcelFileDescriptor.open(file, ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_ONLY)
    }

    override fun insert(uri: Uri, values: ContentValues?): Uri? {
        throw UnsupportedOperationException("読み取り専用プロバイダです")
    }

    override fun delete(uri: Uri, selection: String?, selectionArgs: Array<out String>?): Int {
        throw UnsupportedOperationException("読み取り専用プロバイダです")
    }

    override fun update(
        uri: Uri,
        values: ContentValues?,
        selection: String?,
        selectionArgs: Array<out String>?
    ): Int {
        throw UnsupportedOperationException("読み取り専用プロバイダです")
    }

    private fun resolveSafeFile(uri: Uri): File? {
        val ctx = context ?: return null
        val baseDir = ctx.getExternalFilesDir("runs") ?: return null
        val segments = uri.pathSegments
        if (segments.size < 3 || segments[0] != "runs") return null
        val runDir = segments[1]
        val fileName = segments[2]
        if (runDir.contains("..") || fileName.contains("..")) return null
        val target = File(File(baseDir, runDir), fileName)
        val baseCanonical = baseDir.canonicalPath
        val targetCanonical = target.canonicalPath
        if (!targetCanonical.startsWith(baseCanonical + File.separator)) {
            return null
        }
        return if (target.exists() && target.isFile) target else null
    }
}

/**
 * 走行記録（CSV・マップPNG・JSON・サマリMD）の端末保存、Google ドライブ共有、
 * および GAS Webhook によるバックグラウンド自動転送を管理する。
 */
class LogStorageManager(private val activity: Activity) {

    private val prefs by lazy {
        activity.getSharedPreferences("phone_robot_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    }

    fun getWebhookUrl(): String = prefs.getString(KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, "") ?: ""

    fun setWebhookUrl(url: String) {
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, url.trim()).apply()
    }

    fun saveRunBundle(call: MethodCall, result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        val rawRunId = call.argument<String>("runId")?.trim().orEmpty()
        val safeRunId = rawRunId.replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]"), "")
        if (safeRunId.isEmpty()) {
            result.error("INVALID_ARGS", "runId が空または不正です", null)
            return
        }
        val rawFiles = call.argument<Map<*, *>>("files")
        if (rawFiles == null || rawFiles.isEmpty()) {
            result.error("INVALID_ARGS", "保存対象ファイルがありません", null)
            return
        }
        val autoDriveSync = call.argument<Boolean>("autoDriveSync") ?: true
        val openDriveSheet = call.argument<Boolean>("openDriveSheet") ?: true

        val runsRoot = activity.getExternalFilesDir("runs")
        if (runsRoot == null) {
            result.error("IO_ERROR", "外部ストレージのアプリ領域を取得できません", null)
            return
        }
        val runDir = File(runsRoot, "run_$safeRunId")
        if (!runDir.exists()) {
            runDir.mkdirs()
        }

        val byteFiles = LinkedHashMap<String, ByteArray>()
        try {
            for ((k, v) in rawFiles) {
                val rawName = k?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
                val safeName = File(rawName).name
                if (safeName.isEmpty() || safeName.contains("..")) continue
                val bytes = when (v) {
                    is ByteArray -> v
                    is String -> v.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
                    else -> null
                } ?: continue
                File(runDir, safeName).writeBytes(bytes)
                byteFiles[safeName] = bytes
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            result.error("IO_ERROR", "端末保存に失敗しました: ${e.message}", null)
            return
        }

        // Android 10+ 端末からファイルアプリで見やすい Download/PhoneRobot/ にも複製する。
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            for ((name, bytes) in byteFiles) {
                saveToMediaStoreDownloads(safeRunId, name, bytes)
            }
            val mp4File = File(runDir, "camera_recording_$safeRunId.mp4")
            if (mp4File.exists() && mp4File.isFile && mp4File.length() > 0L) {
                copyFileToMediaStoreDownloads(safeRunId, mp4File)
            }
        }

        val webhookUrl = getWebhookUrl().trim()
        if (autoDriveSync && webhookUrl.startsWith("https://")) {
            Thread {
                val uploaded = postToWebhook(webhookUrl, safeRunId, byteFiles)
                activity.runOnUiThread {
                    result.success(
                        mapOf(
                            "savedDir" to runDir.absolutePath,
                            "driveAction" to "webhook_sent",
                            "ok" to uploaded
                        )
                    )
                }
            }.start()
            return
        }

        if (autoDriveSync && openDriveSheet) {
            val shared = shareRunToDriveInternal(safeRunId)
            result.success(
                mapOf(
                    "savedDir" to runDir.absolutePath,
                    "driveAction" to "intent_opened",
                    "ok" to shared
                )
            )
            return
        }

        result.success(
            mapOf(
                "savedDir" to runDir.absolutePath,
                "driveAction" to "local_only",
                "ok" to true
            )
        )
    }

    fun shareRunToDriveInternal(runId: String?): Boolean {
        val runsRoot = activity.getExternalFilesDir("runs") ?: return false
        val targetDir = if (!runId.isNullOrBlank()) {
            val safe = runId.trim().replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]"), "")
            val candidate = File(runsRoot, "run_$safe")
            if (candidate.exists() && candidate.isDirectory) candidate else null
        } else {
            runsRoot.listFiles()
                ?.filter { it.isDirectory && it.name.startsWith("run_") }
                ?.maxByOrNull { it.lastModified() }
        } ?: return false

        val files = targetDir.listFiles()?.filter { it.isFile }?.sortedBy { it.name }.orEmpty()
        if (files.isEmpty()) return false

        val authority = "${activity.packageName}.logprovider"
        val uris = ArrayList<Uri>(files.size)
        for (file in files) {
            uris.add(Uri.parse("content://$authority/runs/${targetDir.name}/${Uri.encode(file.name)}"))
        }

        val baseIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE).apply {
            type = "*/*"
            putParcelableArrayListExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uris)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "phone-robot ${targetDir.name}")
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "phone-robot 走行記録 (${targetDir.name})")
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        }

        val drivePkg = "com.google.android.apps.docs"
        val driveIntent = Intent(baseIntent).setPackage(drivePkg)
        val pm = activity.packageManager
        return try {
            if (driveIntent.resolveActivity(pm) != null) {
                for (u in uris) {
                    activity.grantUriPermission(drivePkg, u, Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                }
                activity.startActivity(driveIntent)
            } else {
                activity.startActivity(
                    Intent.createChooser(baseIntent, "走行記録を Google ドライブへ保存")
                )
            }
            true
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun saveToMediaStoreDownloads(runId: String, fileName: String, bytes: ByteArray) {
        writeToMediaStoreDownloads(runId, fileName) { out ->
            out.write(bytes)
        }
    }

    private fun copyFileToMediaStoreDownloads(runId: String, file: File) {
        writeToMediaStoreDownloads(runId, file.name) { out ->
            FileInputStream(file).use { input ->
                input.copyTo(out)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun writeToMediaStoreDownloads(
        runId: String,
        fileName: String,
        writer: (java.io.OutputStream) -> Unit
    ) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) return
        try {
            val resolver = activity.contentResolver
            val relPath = "${Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS}/PhoneRobot/run_$runId"
            val values = ContentValues().apply {
                put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, fileName)
                put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, mimeTypeFor(fileName))
                put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, relPath)
            }
            val uri = resolver.insert(MediaStore.Downloads.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, values) ?: return
            resolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use(writer)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // Download 領域への複製失敗時でもアプリ領域への保存は生かす。
        }
    }

    private fun postToWebhook(
        webhookUrl: String,
        runId: String,
        files: Map<String, ByteArray>
    ): Boolean {
        val fileArray = JSONArray()
        for ((name, bytes) in files) {
            fileArray.put(
                JSONObject().apply {
                    put("name", name)
                    put("mimeType", mimeTypeFor(name))
                    put("base64", Base64.encodeToString(bytes, Base64.NO_WRAP))
                }
            )
        }
        val payload = JSONObject().apply {
            put("runId", runId)
            put("files", fileArray)
        }.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)

        var conn: HttpURLConnection? = null
        return try {
            val url = URL(webhookUrl)
            conn = (url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                requestMethod = "POST"
                connectTimeout = 15000
                readTimeout = 30000
                doOutput = true
                instanceFollowRedirects = false
                setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=UTF-8")
            }
            conn.outputStream.use { it.write(payload) }
            val code = conn.responseCode
            // GAS WebApp は doPost 実行後に 302 で結果 URL へ転送するため 200..399 を成功とする。
            if (code in 300..399) {
                val redirectUrl = conn.getHeaderField("Location")
                if (!redirectUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    followRedirectGet(redirectUrl)
                } else {
                    true
                }
            } else {
                code in 200..299
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        } finally {
            conn?.disconnect()
        }
    }

    private fun followRedirectGet(redirectUrl: String): Boolean {
        var conn: HttpURLConnection? = null
        return try {
            conn = (URL(redirectUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                requestMethod = "GET"
                connectTimeout = 15000
                readTimeout = 20000
            }
            conn.responseCode in 200..399
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // 最初の POST 時点ですでに doPost が実行されているためフォールバックは true を返さない。
            false
        } finally {
            conn?.disconnect()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val KEY_WEBHOOK_URL = "gas_webhook_url"

        fun mimeTypeFor(fileName: String): String {
            val lower = fileName.lowercase()
            return when {
                lower.endsWith(".csv") -> "text/csv"
                lower.endsWith(".png") -> "image/png"
                lower.endsWith(".json") -> "application/json"
                lower.endsWith(".md") -> "text/markdown"
                lower.endsWith(".mp4") -> "video/mp4"
                lower.endsWith(".apk") -> "application/vnd.android.package-archive"
                else -> "application/octet-stream"
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## File: `flutter/app/android/app/src/main/kotlin/dev/fumiproject/phone_robot_app/MainActivity.kt`
```kt
package dev.fumiproject.phone_robot_app

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.view.WindowManager
import io.flutter.embedding.android.FlutterActivity
import io.flutter.embedding.engine.FlutterEngine
import io.flutter.plugin.common.EventChannel
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

/**
 * プラットフォームチャネルの配線だけを行う薄い Activity。
 * 実処理は ArCoreManager（自己位置＋Depth）と SerialManager（ESP32）にある。
 *
 * チャネル一覧:
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/arcore"  : startPoseTracking / stopPoseTracking / getCameraTextureId / startMp4Recording / stopMp4Recording
 *  - EventChannel  "phone_robot/arcore/pose"     : {x, y, heading}
 *  - EventChannel  "phone_robot/arcore/tracking" : 状態文字列
 *  - EventChannel  "phone_robot/depth"           : {ranges: [21本]}
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/serial"  : listDevices / connect / disconnect / send
 *  - EventChannel  "phone_robot/serial/state"    : リンク状態文字列
 *  - EventChannel  "phone_robot/serial/rx"       : 受信テキスト
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/system"  : setKeepScreenOn（実機モード中の画面常時点灯、R4）
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/storage" : saveRunBundle / shareRunToDrive / getWebhookUrl / setWebhookUrl
 */
class MainActivity : FlutterActivity() {

    private var arCore: ArCoreManager? = null
    private var serial: SerialManager? = null
    private var logStorage: LogStorageManager? = null

    override fun configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine: FlutterEngine) {
        super.configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine)
        val messenger = flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger

        val ar = ArCoreManager(this, flutterEngine.renderer)
        arCore = ar
        val se = SerialManager(this)
        serial = se
        val ls = LogStorageManager(this)
        logStorage = ls

        MethodChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/arcore").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "startPoseTracking" -> ar.start(result)
                "stopPoseTracking" -> {
                    ar.stop()
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "getCameraTextureId" -> result.success(ar.getCameraTextureId())
                "startMp4Recording" -> result.success(
                    ar.startMp4Recording(call.argument<String>("runId") ?: "")
                )
                "stopMp4Recording" -> result.success(ar.stopMp4Recording())
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }
        EventChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/arcore/pose").setStreamHandler(ar.poseHandler)
        EventChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/arcore/tracking")
            .setStreamHandler(ar.trackingHandler)
        EventChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/depth").setStreamHandler(ar.depthHandler)

        MethodChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/serial").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "listDevices" -> result.success(se.listDevices())
                "connect" -> se.connect(
                    call.argument<Int>("deviceId"),
                    call.argument<Int>("baudRate") ?: 115200,
                    result
                )
                "disconnect" -> {
                    se.disconnect()
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "send" -> result.success(se.send(call.argument<String>("text") ?: ""))
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }
        EventChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/serial/state").setStreamHandler(se.stateHandler)
        EventChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/serial/rx").setStreamHandler(se.rxHandler)

        // 実機モード中の画面常時点灯（R4）。消灯が One UI のプロセス
        // 凍結の引き金になるため、Dart 側の KeepAwake から切り替える。
        MethodChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/system").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "setKeepScreenOn" -> {
                    val on = call.argument<Boolean>("on") ?: false
                    runOnUiThread {
                        if (on) {
                            window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
                        } else {
                            window.clearFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
                        }
                    }
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "getVersionName" -> {
                    val v = try {
                        packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0).versionName ?: "0.0.0"
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                        "0.0.0"
                    }
                    result.success(v)
                }
                "getAutoStartSensors" -> {
                    val prefs = getSharedPreferences("phone_robot_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                    result.success(prefs.getBoolean("auto_start_sensors", false))
                }
                "setAutoStartSensors" -> {
                    val on = call.argument<Boolean>("on") ?: false
                    getSharedPreferences("phone_robot_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                        .edit()
                        .putBoolean("auto_start_sensors", on)
                        .apply()
                    result.success(true)
                }
                "checkUpdate" -> checkLatestRelease(result)
                "downloadAndInstallApk" -> {
                    val apkUrl = call.argument<String>("url") ?: ""
                    downloadAndInstallApk(apkUrl, result)
                }
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }

        MethodChannel(messenger, "phone_robot/storage").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "saveRunBundle" -> ls.saveRunBundle(call, result)
                "shareRunToDrive" -> result.success(
                    ls.shareRunToDriveInternal(call.argument<String>("runId"))
                )
                "getWebhookUrl" -> result.success(ls.getWebhookUrl())
                "setWebhookUrl" -> {
                    ls.setWebhookUrl(call.argument<String>("url") ?: "")
                    result.success(true)
                }
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(
        requestCode: Int,
        permissions: Array<out String>,
        grantResults: IntArray
    ) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        arCore?.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, grantResults)
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        arCore?.stop()
        serial?.close()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    private fun checkLatestRelease(result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        val currentVersion = try {
            packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0).versionName ?: "0.0.0"
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            "0.0.0"
        }
        Thread {
            var conn: HttpURLConnection? = null
            try {
                val url = URL("https://api.github.com/repos/fumi3333/phone-robot/releases/latest")
                conn = (url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                    requestMethod = "GET"
                    connectTimeout = 10000
                    readTimeout = 10000
                    setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")
                    setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "phone-robot-app")
                }
                if (conn.responseCode !in 200..299) {
                    runOnUiThread { result.success(mapOf("ok" to false, "error" to "HTTP ${conn.responseCode}")) }
                    return@Thread
                }
                val body = conn.inputStream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
                val json = JSONObject(body)
                val tag = json.optString("tag_name", "")
                val latestClean = tag.removePrefix("v")
                val assets = json.optJSONArray("assets")
                var apkUrl = ""
                if (assets != null) {
                    for (i in 0 until assets.length()) {
                        val a = assets.getJSONObject(i)
                        val name = a.optString("name", "")
                        if (name.endsWith(".apk")) {
                            apkUrl = a.optString("browser_download_url", "")
                            if (name.contains("v$latestClean")) break
                        }
                    }
                }
                val hasUpdate = latestClean.isNotEmpty() && compareVersions(latestClean, currentVersion) > 0
                runOnUiThread {
                    result.success(
                        mapOf(
                            "ok" to true,
                            "currentVersion" to currentVersion,
                            "latestVersion" to latestClean,
                            "tag" to tag,
                            "hasUpdate" to hasUpdate,
                            "apkUrl" to apkUrl,
                            "releaseUrl" to json.optString("html_url", "")
                        )
                    )
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                runOnUiThread { result.success(mapOf("ok" to false, "error" to (e.message ?: "error"))) }
            } finally {
                conn?.disconnect()
            }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun downloadAndInstallApk(apkUrl: String, result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        if (apkUrl.isBlank()) {
            result.success(mapOf("ok" to false, "error" to "APK URL が空です"))
            return
        }
        Thread {
            var conn: HttpURLConnection? = null
            try {
                val runsRoot = getExternalFilesDir("runs")
                    ?: throw IllegalStateException("外部キャッシュにアクセスできません")
                val updateDir = File(runsRoot, "update")
                if (!updateDir.exists()) updateDir.mkdirs()
                val apkFile = File(updateDir, "update_latest.apk")

                var currentUrl = apkUrl
                for (redirect in 0..4) {
                    conn = (URL(currentUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                        requestMethod = "GET"
                        connectTimeout = 15000
                        readTimeout = 60000
                        instanceFollowRedirects = false
                        setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "phone-robot-app")
                    }
                    val code = conn.responseCode
                    if (code in 300..399) {
                        val next = conn.getHeaderField("Location")
                        conn.disconnect()
                        if (next.isNullOrBlank()) break
                        currentUrl = next
                    } else {
                        break
                    }
                }
                val activeConn = conn ?: throw IllegalStateException("接続に失敗しました")
                if (activeConn.responseCode !in 200..299) {
                    runOnUiThread {
                        result.success(mapOf("ok" to false, "error" to "HTTP ${activeConn.responseCode}"))
                    }
                    return@Thread
                }
                activeConn.inputStream.use { input ->
                    FileOutputStream(apkFile).use { output ->
                        val buf = ByteArray(16384)
                        while (true) {
                            val n = input.read(buf)
                            if (n <= 0) break
                            output.write(buf, 0, n)
                        }
                    }
                }
                val contentUri = Uri.parse("content://$packageName.logprovider/runs/update/update_latest.apk")
                val installIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                    setDataAndType(contentUri, "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                }
                runOnUiThread {
                    startActivity(installIntent)
                    result.success(mapOf("ok" to true))
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                runOnUiThread {
                    result.success(mapOf("ok" to false, "error" to (e.message ?: "install error")))
                }
            } finally {
                conn?.disconnect()
            }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun compareVersions(a: String, b: String): Int {
        val pa = a.split(".").map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 }
        val pb = b.split(".").map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 }
        val maxLen = maxOf(pa.size, pb.size)
        for (i in 0 until maxLen) {
            val va = if (i < pa.size) pa[i] else 0
            val vb = if (i < pb.size) pb[i] else 0
            if (va != vb) return va.compareTo(vb)
        }
        return 0
    }
}
```

## File: `flutter/app/android/app/src/main/kotlin/dev/fumiproject/phone_robot_app/SerialManager.kt`
```kt
package dev.fumiproject.phone_robot_app

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.hardware.usb.UsbDevice
import android.hardware.usb.UsbManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.hoho.android.usbserial.driver.UsbSerialPort
import com.hoho.android.usbserial.driver.UsbSerialProber
import com.hoho.android.usbserial.util.SerialInputOutputManager
import io.flutter.plugin.common.EventChannel
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

/**
 * USB シリアル（usb-serial-for-android）で ESP32 とつなぐ管理クラス。
 *
 * チャネル契約（Dart: platform/serial_link.dart）:
 *  - MethodChannel "phone_robot/serial":
 *      listDevices -> [{deviceId, vendorId, productId, name}]
 *      connect {deviceId?, baudRate} -> bool
 *      disconnect -> null
 *      send {text} -> bool
 *  - EventChannel "phone_robot/serial/state": "connected" / "connecting" /
 *    "disconnected" / "error"
 *  - EventChannel "phone_robot/serial/rx": 受信テキスト（表示用）
 *
 * 対応チップはライブラリのデフォルトプローバ依存（CH340 / CP210x / FTDI /
 * CDC 等）。ESP32 基板（Lzxuan, CH340C）は VID 0x1A86。
 */
class SerialManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "SerialManager"
        private const val ACTION_USB_PERMISSION =
            "dev.fumiproject.phone_robot_app.USB_PERMISSION"
    }

    private val usbManager = context.getSystemService(Context.USB_SERVICE) as UsbManager

    @Volatile private var port: UsbSerialPort? = null
    @Volatile private var ioManager: SerialInputOutputManager? = null
    @Volatile private var txRunning = false
    private var txThread: Thread? = null
    private val txLock = Object()
    private var pendingPayload: ByteArray? = null
    private var stateSink: EventChannel.EventSink? = null
    private var rxSink: EventChannel.EventSink? = null
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    private var pendingResult: MethodChannel.Result? = null
    private var pendingDevice: UsbDevice? = null
    private var pendingBaud: Int = 115200

    private val permissionReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(ctx: Context, intent: Intent) {
            if (intent.action != ACTION_USB_PERMISSION) return
            val granted =
                intent.getBooleanExtra(UsbManager.EXTRA_PERMISSION_GRANTED, false)
            val result = pendingResult
            val device = pendingDevice
            pendingResult = null
            pendingDevice = null
            if (result == null) return
            if (granted && device != null) {
                result.success(openPort(device, pendingBaud))
            } else {
                pushState("error")
                result.success(false)
            }
        }
    }

    private val detachReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(ctx: Context, intent: Intent) {
            if (intent.action == UsbManager.ACTION_USB_DEVICE_DETACHED) {
                disconnect()
            }
        }
    }

    init {
        ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
            context,
            permissionReceiver,
            IntentFilter(ACTION_USB_PERMISSION),
            ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
        )
        ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
            context,
            detachReceiver,
            IntentFilter(UsbManager.ACTION_USB_DEVICE_DETACHED),
            ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
        )
    }

    val stateHandler = object : EventChannel.StreamHandler {
        override fun onListen(arguments: Any?, events: EventChannel.EventSink?) {
            stateSink = events
        }
        override fun onCancel(arguments: Any?) { stateSink = null }
    }

    val rxHandler = object : EventChannel.StreamHandler {
        override fun onListen(arguments: Any?, events: EventChannel.EventSink?) {
            rxSink = events
        }
        override fun onCancel(arguments: Any?) { rxSink = null }
    }

    fun listDevices(): List<Map<String, Any>> {
        val drivers = UsbSerialProber.getDefaultProber().findAllDrivers(usbManager)
        return drivers.map { driver ->
            mapOf(
                "deviceId" to driver.device.deviceId,
                "vendorId" to driver.device.vendorId,
                "productId" to driver.device.productId,
                "name" to (driver.device.productName ?: driver.device.deviceName ?: "USB serial")
            )
        }
    }

    fun connect(deviceId: Int?, baudRate: Int, result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        pushState("connecting")
        val drivers = UsbSerialProber.getDefaultProber().findAllDrivers(usbManager)
        val driver = drivers.firstOrNull { deviceId == null || it.device.deviceId == deviceId }
        if (driver == null) {
            pushState("error")
            result.success(false)
            return
        }
        val device = driver.device
        if (!usbManager.hasPermission(device)) {
            pendingResult = result
            pendingDevice = device
            pendingBaud = baudRate
            val intent = Intent(ACTION_USB_PERMISSION).apply {
                setPackage(context.packageName)
            }
            val flags =
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                    PendingIntent.FLAG_MUTABLE
                } else {
                    0
                }
            val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(context, 0, intent, flags)
            usbManager.requestPermission(device, pendingIntent)
            return
        }
        result.success(openPort(device, baudRate))
    }

    private fun openPort(device: UsbDevice, baudRate: Int): Boolean {
        val drivers = UsbSerialProber.getDefaultProber().findAllDrivers(usbManager)
        val driver = drivers.firstOrNull { it.device.deviceId == device.deviceId }
            ?: run {
                pushState("error")
                return false
            }
        val connection = usbManager.openDevice(device)
            ?: run {
                pushState("error")
                return false
            }
        val p = driver.ports[0]
        return try {
            p.open(connection)
            p.setParameters(baudRate, 8, UsbSerialPort.STOPBITS_1, UsbSerialPort.PARITY_NONE)
            try {
                p.dtr = false
                p.rts = false
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
            port = p
            val io = SerialInputOutputManager(p, object : SerialInputOutputManager.Listener {
                override fun onNewData(data: ByteArray) {
                    val text = String(data, Charsets.UTF_8)
                    mainHandler.post { rxSink?.success(text) }
                }

                override fun onRunError(e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "serial io error: ${e.message}")
                    pushState("error")
                }
            })
            ioManager = io
            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().submit(io)
            startTxThread()
            pushState("connected")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "open failed: ${e.message}")
            pushState("error")
            false
        }
    }

    private fun startTxThread() {
        stopTxThread()
        txRunning = true
        val t = Thread({
            while (txRunning) {
                var payload: ByteArray?
                synchronized(txLock) {
                    while (txRunning && pendingPayload == null) {
                        try {
                            txLock.wait()
                        } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
                            return@Thread
                        }
                    }
                    payload = pendingPayload
                    pendingPayload = null
                }
                val bytes = payload ?: continue
                val p = port ?: continue
                try {
                    // UI スレッドをブロックせず最新指令だけを送る（遅延・キュー詰まり防止）
                    p.write(bytes, 150)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "write failed: ${e.message}")
                }
            }
        }, "SerialTxThread")
        t.isDaemon = true
        t.start()
        txThread = t
    }

    private fun stopTxThread() {
        txRunning = false
        synchronized(txLock) {
            pendingPayload = null
            txLock.notifyAll()
        }
        try {
            txThread?.interrupt()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        }
        txThread = null
    }

    fun send(text: String): Boolean {
        if (port == null) return false
        val bytes = text.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        synchronized(txLock) {
            pendingPayload = bytes
            txLock.notifyAll()
        }
        return true
    }

    fun disconnect() {
        stopTxThread()
        try {
            ioManager?.stop()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
        }
        ioManager = null
        try {
            port?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
        }
        port = null
        pushState("disconnected")
    }

    fun close() {
        disconnect()
        try {
            context.unregisterReceiver(permissionReceiver)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
        }
        try {
            context.unregisterReceiver(detachReceiver)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
        }
    }

    private fun pushState(state: String) {
        mainHandler.post { stateSink?.success(state) }
    }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'real_page.dart';

void main() {
  runApp(const PhoneRobotApp());
}

/// phone-robot マッピング＆ラジコンテストアプリ。
class PhoneRobotApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const PhoneRobotApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'phone-robot',
      theme: ThemeData(
        colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
          seedColor: const Color(0xFF111827),
          brightness: Brightness.light,
        ),
        scaffoldBackgroundColor: Colors.white,
        useMaterial3: true,
      ),
      home: const HomePage(),
    );
  }
}

class HomePage extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomePage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return const RealPage();
  }
}
```
