```dart
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:phone_robot_core/phone_robot_core.dart';

/// 占有格子地図および直感オーバーレイの描画。
///
/// [followRobot] が true かつ自己位置があるときは現在地を中心に拡大表示（1m/2mリング付き）し、
/// 周囲の障害物（赤）と通路（白）を直感的に見せる。false のときは全体図を表示する。
class MapPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final Float32List logOdds;
  final int gridW;
  final int gridH;
  final Cell robot;
  final Cell? goal;
  final List<Cell>? path;
  final Pose? robotPose;
  final Scan? latestScan;
  final double? targetHeading;
  final double? targetDistM;
  final MappingParams mappingParams;
  final bool followRobot;
  final double viewRadiusM;
  final Offset? cameraCenterWorld;

  const MapPainter({
    required this.logOdds,
    required this.gridW,
    required this.gridH,
    required this.robot,
    this.goal,
    this.path,
    this.robotPose,
    this.latestScan,
    this.targetHeading,
    this.targetDistM,
    this.mappingParams = const MappingParams(),
    this.followRobot = true,
    this.viewRadiusM = 2.5,
    this.cameraCenterWorld,
  });

  /// 画面タップ座標を地図グリッドのセルに変換する。範囲外なら null。
  static Cell? cellAtTap({
    required Offset tap,
    required Size size,
    required MappingParams params,
    Pose? robotPose,
    bool followRobot = false,
    double viewRadiusM = 2.5,
    Offset? cameraCenterWorld,
  }) {
    if (size.width <= 0 || size.height <= 0) return null;
    final int r;
    final int c;
    final Offset? camCenter = (followRobot && robotPose != null)
        ? Offset(robotPose.x, robotPose.y)
        : cameraCenterWorld;
    if (camCenter != null) {
      final pxPerM = math.min(size.width, size.height) / (2.0 * viewRadiusM);
      final wx = camCenter.dx + (tap.dx - size.width / 2.0) / pxPerM;
      final wy = camCenter.dy - (tap.dy - size.height / 2.0) / pxPerM;
      c = (wx / params.cellSize).floor();
      r = (wy / params.cellSize).floor();
    } else {
      final cw = size.width / params.gridW;
      final ch = size.height / params.gridH;
      c = (tap.dx / cw).floor();
      final rFromTop = (tap.dy / ch).floor();
      r = params.gridH - 1 - rFromTop;
    }
    if (r < 0 || r >= params.gridH || c < 0 || c >= params.gridW) return null;
    return (r: r, c: c);
  }

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    if (size.width <= 0 || size.height <= 0) return;
    canvas.save();
    canvas.clipRect(Offset.zero & size);

    final rp = robotPose;
    final Offset? camCenter = (followRobot && rp != null)
        ? Offset(rp.x, rp.y)
        : cameraCenterWorld;
    final useCameraView = camCenter != null;
    final cs = mappingParams.cellSize;

    final double cw;
    final double ch;
    final double pxPerM;
    late final Offset Function(double wx, double wy) worldToCanvas;
    late final Offset Function(Cell cell) centerOf;

    if (useCameraView) {
      pxPerM = math.min(size.width, size.height) / (2.0 * viewRadiusM);
      cw = cs * pxPerM;
      ch = cw;
      final cx = size.width / 2.0;
      final cy = size.height / 2.0;
      worldToCanvas = (wx, wy) =>
          Offset(cx + (wx - camCenter.dx) * pxPerM, cy - (wy - camCenter.dy) * pxPerM);
      centerOf = (cell) =>
          worldToCanvas((cell.c + 0.5) * cs, (cell.r + 0.5) * cs);
    } else {
      cw = size.width / gridW;
      ch = size.height / gridH;
      pxPerM = math.min(cw, ch) / cs;
      worldToCanvas = (wx, wy) =>
          Offset((wx / cs) * cw, (gridH - wy / cs) * ch);
      centerOf = (cell) =>
          Offset((cell.c + 0.5) * cw, (gridH - 1 - cell.r + 0.5) * ch);
    }

    final unknownPaint = Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFE5E7EB);
    final freePaint = Paint()..color = Colors.white;
    final occPaint = Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFEF4444);

    canvas.drawRect(Offset.zero & size, unknownPaint);

    // 可視範囲だけを求め、行単位で連続セルをまとめて描画して負荷を大幅に下げる
    var minR = 0;
    var maxR = gridH - 1;
    var minC = 0;
    var maxC = gridW - 1;
    if (useCameraView) {
      final halfW = (size.width / 2.0) / pxPerM;
      final halfH = (size.height / 2.0) / pxPerM;
      minC = ((camCenter.dx - halfW) / cs).floor().clamp(0, gridW - 1);
      maxC = ((camCenter.dx + halfW) / cs).ceil().clamp(0, gridW - 1);
      minR = ((camCenter.dy - halfH) / cs).floor().clamp(0, gridH - 1);
      maxR = ((camCenter.dy + halfH) / cs).ceil().clamp(0, gridH - 1);
    }

    for (var r = minR; r <= maxR; r++) {
      final rowBase = r * gridW;
      final top = useCameraView
          ? size.height / 2.0 - ((r + 1) * cs - camCenter.dy) * pxPerM
          : (gridH - 1 - r) * ch;
      var c = minC;
      while (c <= maxC) {
        final v = logOdds[rowBase + c];
        if (v == 0) {
          c++;
          continue;
        }
        final isFree = v < 0;
        var runEnd = c + 1;
        while (runEnd <= maxC) {
          final nv = logOdds[rowBase + runEnd];
          if (nv == 0 || (nv < 0) != isFree) break;
          runEnd++;
        }
        final left = useCameraView
            ? size.width / 2.0 + (c * cs - camCenter.dx) * pxPerM
            : c * cw;
        final span = (runEnd - c) * cw;
        canvas.drawRect(
          Rect.fromLTWH(left, top, span + 0.5, ch + 0.5),
          isFree ? freePaint : occPaint,
        );
        c = runEnd;
      }
    }

    if (useCameraView && rp != null) {
      final center = worldToCanvas(rp.x, rp.y);
      final ringPaint = Paint()
        ..color = const Color(0x5564748B)
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 1.0;
      for (final distM in [1.0, 2.0]) {
        final radiusPx = distM * pxPerM;
        canvas.drawCircle(center, radiusPx, ringPaint);
      }
    }

    if (rp != null) {
      // カメラが現在向いている視野角（±halfFov、半径 1.5m）を薄い扇形で表示する
      final fovCenter = worldToCanvas(rp.x, rp.y);
      final coneR = 1.5 * pxPerM;
      if (coneR >= 6.0) {
        final halfFov = mappingParams.fovRad / 2.0;
        final fovRect = Rect.fromCircle(center: fovCenter, radius: coneR);
        canvas.drawArc(
          fovRect,
          -rp.heading - halfFov,
          mappingParams.fovRad,
          true,
          Paint()..color = const Color.fromARGB(28, 14, 165, 233),
        );
        canvas.drawArc(
          fovRect,
          -rp.heading - halfFov,
          mappingParams.fovRad,
          true,
          Paint()
            ..color = const Color.fromARGB(95, 2, 132, 199)
            ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
            ..strokeWidth = 1.2,
        );
      }
    }

    final scan = latestScan;
    if (scan != null && scan.ranges.isNotEmpty) {
      final offsets = rayOffsets(mappingParams);
      final rayMeasuredPaint = Paint()
        ..color = const Color.fromARGB(130, 0, 188, 212)
        ..strokeWidth = 1.4;
      final rayOpenPaint = Paint()
        ..color = const Color.fromARGB(45, 0, 188, 212)
        ..strokeWidth = 1.0;
      final hitDotPaint = Paint()
        ..color = const Color.fromARGB(210, 220, 38, 38);
      final origin = worldToCanvas(scan.pose.x, scan.pose.y);
      final n = math.min(offsets.length, scan.ranges.length);
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        final r = scan.ranges[i];
        if (r.isNaN || r <= 0) continue;
        final measured = r.isFinite;
        final dist = measured
            ? math.min(r, mappingParams.maxRange)
            : math.min(2.5, mappingParams.maxRange);
        final ang = scan.pose.heading + offsets[i];
        final end = worldToCanvas(
          scan.pose.x + math.cos(ang) * dist,
          scan.pose.y + math.sin(ang) * dist,
        );
        canvas.drawLine(
          origin,
          end,
          measured ? rayMeasuredPaint : rayOpenPaint,
        );
        if (measured) {
          canvas.drawCircle(end, 2.5, hitDotPaint);
        }
      }
    }

    final route = path;
    if (route != null && route.length > 1) {
      final linePaint = Paint()
        ..color = Colors.green
        ..strokeWidth = 2.4
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;
      final line = Path()
        ..moveTo(centerOf(route.first).dx, centerOf(route.first).dy);
      for (final cell in route.skip(1)) {
        final o = centerOf(cell);
        line.lineTo(o.dx, o.dy);
      }
      canvas.drawPath(line, linePaint);
    }

    final th = targetHeading;
    if (rp != null && th != null) {
      final dist = (targetDistM ?? 1.2).clamp(0.3, mappingParams.maxRange);
      final startPt = worldToCanvas(rp.x, rp.y);
      final endPt = worldToCanvas(
        rp.x + math.cos(th) * dist,
        rp.y + math.sin(th) * dist,
      );
      final targetPaint = Paint()
        ..color = const Color.fromARGB(230, 0, 200, 83)
        ..strokeWidth = 3.0
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
      canvas.drawLine(startPt, endPt, targetPaint);
      canvas.drawCircle(
        endPt,
        4.0,
        Paint()..color = const Color.fromARGB(230, 0, 200, 83),
      );
    }

    final g = goal;
    if (g != null) {
      canvas.drawCircle(
        centerOf(g),
        math.max(cw * 1.4, 5.5),
        Paint()..color = Colors.blue,
      );
    }

    final rc = rp != null ? worldToCanvas(rp.x, rp.y) : centerOf(robot);
    final robotRadius = math.max(cw * 1.2, 7.5).clamp(7.5, 13.0).toDouble();
    canvas.drawCircle(
      rc,
      robotRadius + 2.5,
      Paint()..color = Colors.white,
    );
    canvas.drawCircle(
      rc,
      robotRadius,
      Paint()..color = const Color(0xFF1A73E8),
    );
    if (rp != null) {
      final tip = Offset(
        rc.dx + math.cos(rp.heading) * (robotRadius * 1.7),
        rc.dy - math.sin(rp.heading) * (robotRadius * 1.7),
      );
      canvas.drawLine(
        rc,
        tip,
        Paint()
          ..color = Colors.white
          ..strokeWidth = 2.5
          ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round,
      );
    }
    canvas.restore();
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant MapPainter oldDelegate) => true;
}
```

## File: `flutter/app/lib/platform/depth_source.dart`
```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:phone_robot_core/phone_robot_core.dart';

/// 距離スキャンの出所（SPEC §5.2 の差し替え点）。
///
/// 実機では ARCore Depth API の深度画像を入力し、次の手順で
/// Scan.ranges（21 本）を作る（21 本化はネイティブ側 ArCoreManager が担当）:
/// 1. 深度画像の中央の帯（車両の進路高さに相当する行）を取り、
///    水平方向に 21 点を等間隔サンプリングする。
/// 2. 生深度ではなく Raw Depth（信頼度付き）を使い、信頼度の低い点は
///    「計測なし（無限遠）」として扱う。
/// 3. 深度画像の画角と FOV 60° の対応は機種ごとのキャリブレーション値。
///
/// 白壁・暗所では深度が外れる前提で、「計測なし」を異常扱いしない。
/// 深度が使えない環境ではネイティブ側が全レイ「計測なし」で送り続ける
/// （フォールバック）。その場合スキャンは地図を更新しない。
abstract class DepthSource {
  /// [currentPose] はスキャン時点の believed pose を返す関数。
  /// スキャンの向きは「実際に深度カメラが向いている向き」でなければ
  /// ならない（SPEC §5.3。移動方向で代用しない）。
  Stream<Scan> scans(Pose Function() currentPose);
  Future<void> stop();
}

/// プラットフォームチャネル経由の Depth API 実装。
///
/// ネイティブ側が信頼度フィルタ済みの 21 本の距離 [m] を送る契約。
/// 負値または 0 以下は「計測なし（ToF 信頼度不足）」を表し、`double.nan` へ
/// 変換して占有格子更新をスキップさせる（開空間の `double.infinity` と区別）。
class MethodChannelDepthSource implements DepthSource {
  static const EventChannel _events = EventChannel('phone_robot/depth');

  final MappingParams params;

  const MethodChannelDepthSource({this.params = const MappingParams()});

  @override
  Stream<Scan> scans(Pose Function() currentPose) {
    return _events.receiveBroadcastStream().map((dynamic e) {
      final m = e as Map<dynamic, dynamic>;
      final raw = m['ranges'] as List<dynamic>;
      final ranges = Float32List(params.nRays);
      for (var i = 0; i < params.nRays && i < raw.length; i++) {
        final v = (raw[i] as num).toDouble();
        ranges[i] = (v.isNaN || v <= 0) ? double.nan : v;
      }
      return Scan(currentPose(), ranges);
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> stop() async {}
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

/// 画面の常時点灯（R4 対策）。
///
/// 実機モード中は画面消灯が One UI のプロセス凍結の引き金になるため、
/// センサー開始中はネイティブ側で FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON を立てる。
/// チャネルが無い環境（テスト等）では静かに何もしない。
class KeepAwake {
  static const MethodChannel _channel = MethodChannel('phone_robot/system');

  static Future<void> setEnabled(bool on) async {
    try {
      await _channel.invokeMethod<void>('setKeepScreenOn', {'on': on});
    } catch (_) {
      // ネイティブ側未実装の環境では無視する（安全側の補助策のため）。
    }
  }
}
```

## File: `flutter/app/lib/platform/pose_source.dart`
```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:phone_robot_core/phone_robot_core.dart';

/// 自己位置の出所（SPEC §5.1 の差し替え点）。
///
/// 実機では ARCore Motion Tracking のカメラポーズを使う。ARCore の
/// 座標系（右手系・y 上・カメラ後方 +z）は SPEC §1 の座標系と異なる
/// ため、ネイティブ側で一度だけ変換し、Dart 側はすべて SPEC §1 の
/// 座標で統一する。変換はセッション開始時の初期位置・初期方位で固定
/// する（ネイティブ側 ArCoreManager の契約）。
///
/// 注意: ARCore の自己位置にもドリフトがある（走行距離の 1〜5% が目安）。
/// 将来の対策（ToF 実測による位置補正、ループクロージャ）は Pose に
/// 補正量を差し込む形で拡張する想定（SPEC §5.1）。
abstract class PoseSource {
  Future<void> start();
  Stream<Pose> get poses;

  /// ARCore のトラッキング状態。値はネイティブ側の文字列そのまま:
  /// 'TRACKING' / 'PAUSED' / 'STOPPED' / 'UNSUPPORTED' / 'ERROR'。
  Stream<String> get trackingStates;

  /// カメラ映像を表示するための Android SurfaceTexture ID。未対応時は null。
  Future<int?> getCameraTextureId();

  /// ARCore の MP4 同時録画を開始し、保存予定パスを返す。未対応時は null。
  Future<String?> startMp4Recording(String runId);

  /// ARCore の MP4 同時録画を停止し、保存済みファイルパスを返す。未録画時は null。
  Future<String?> stopMp4Recording();

  Future<void> stop();
}

/// プラットフォームチャネル経由の ARCore 実装。
///
/// ネイティブ側（Android / ArCoreManager.kt）が SPEC §1 の座標系へ
/// 変換済みの {x, y, heading} を EventChannel で送る契約。
class MethodChannelPoseSource implements PoseSource {
  static const MethodChannel _method = MethodChannel('phone_robot/arcore');
  static const EventChannel _poseEvents = EventChannel(
    'phone_robot/arcore/pose',
  );
  static const EventChannel _trackingEvents = EventChannel(
    'phone_robot/arcore/tracking',
  );

  Stream<Pose>? _poseStream;
  Stream<String>? _trackingStream;

  @override
  Future<void> start() => _method.invokeMethod<void>('startPoseTracking');

  @override
  Stream<Pose> get poses {
    return _poseStream ??= _poseEvents.receiveBroadcastStream().map((
      dynamic e,
    ) {
      final m = e as Map<dynamic, dynamic>;
      return Pose(
        (m['x'] as num).toDouble(),
        (m['y'] as num).toDouble(),
        (m['heading'] as num).toDouble(),
      );
    });
  }

  @override
  Stream<String> get trackingStates {
    return _trackingStream ??= _trackingEvents.receiveBroadcastStream().map(
      (dynamic e) => e.toString(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<int?> getCameraTextureId() async {
    try {
      final id = await _method.invokeMethod<int>('getCameraTextureId');
      if (id == null || id < 0) return null;
      return id;
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<String?> startMp4Recording(String runId) async {
    try {
      return await _method.invokeMethod<String>('startMp4Recording', {
        'runId': runId,
      });
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<String?> stopMp4Recording() async {
    try {
      return await _method.invokeMethod<String>('stopMp4Recording');
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return null;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> stop() => _method.invokeMethod<void>('stopPoseTracking');
}
```

## File: `flutter/app/lib/platform/run_storage.dart`
```dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:phone_robot_core/phone_robot_core.dart';

/// 走行軌跡の1点（ARCore グリッド座標）。
typedef TrajectoryPoint = ({int elapsedMs, double x, double y, double heading});

/// 送信した1つの命令（または同一指令の連続区間）に対する実車・センサー変化の要約。
class CommandEffectSegment {
  final int index;
  final int startElapsedMs;
  final int endElapsedMs;
  final int drivePwm;
  final int steerPwm;
  final String actionLabel;
  final double? startX;
  final double? startY;
  final double? startHeadingDeg;
  final double? endX;
  final double? endY;
  final double? endHeadingDeg;
  final double? dxM;
  final double? dyM;
  final double? distanceMovedM;
  final double? dHeadingDeg;
  final double? startFrontMinM;
  final double? endFrontMinM;
  final double? dFrontMinM;

  const CommandEffectSegment({
    required this.index,
    required this.startElapsedMs,
    required this.endElapsedMs,
    required this.drivePwm,
    required this.steerPwm,
    required this.actionLabel,
    this.startX,
    this.startY,
    this.startHeadingDeg,
    this.endX,
    this.endY,
    this.endHeadingDeg,
    this.dxM,
    this.dyM,
    this.distanceMovedM,
    this.dHeadingDeg,
    this.startFrontMinM,
    this.endFrontMinM,
    this.dFrontMinM,
  });

  int get durationMs => math.max(0, endElapsedMs - startElapsedMs);

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'index': index,
    'startElapsedMs': startElapsedMs,
    'endElapsedMs': endElapsedMs,
    'durationMs': durationMs,
    'drivePwm': drivePwm,
    'steerPwm': steerPwm,
    'actionLabel': actionLabel,
    'startX': _roundOpt(startX, 4),
    'startY': _roundOpt(startY, 4),
    'startHeadingDeg': _roundOpt(startHeadingDeg, 2),
    'endX': _roundOpt(endX, 4),
    'endY': _roundOpt(endY, 4),
    'endHeadingDeg': _roundOpt(endHeadingDeg, 2),
    'dxM': _roundOpt(dxM, 4),
    'dyM': _roundOpt(dyM, 4),
    'distanceMovedM': _roundOpt(distanceMovedM, 4),
    'dHeadingDeg': _roundOpt(dHeadingDeg, 2),
    'startFrontMinM': _roundOpt(startFrontMinM, 3),
    'endFrontMinM': _roundOpt(endFrontMinM, 3),
    'dFrontMinM': _roundOpt(dFrontMinM, 3),
  };

  static double? _roundOpt(double? v, int digits) =>
      v == null ? null : double.parse(v.toStringAsFixed(digits));
}

String classifyCommandAction(int drivePwm, int steerPwm) {
  if (drivePwm == 0 && steerPwm == 0) return '停止';
  if (drivePwm == 0 && steerPwm < 0) return '先当て左';
  if (drivePwm == 0 && steerPwm > 0) return '先当て右';
  if (drivePwm > 0 && steerPwm < 0) return '左旋回';
  if (drivePwm > 0 && steerPwm > 0) return '右旋回';
  if (drivePwm > 0) return '前進';
  if (drivePwm < 0 && steerPwm < 0) return '後退左';
  if (drivePwm < 0 && steerPwm > 0) return '後退右';
  return '後退';
}

/// 走行終了時（または手動記録停止時）に出力される記録ファイル一式。
class RunBundle {
  final String runId;
  final String mode;
  final DateTime startedAt;
  final DateTime endedAt;
  final String csvContent;
  final String commandEffectsCsvContent;
  final List<CommandEffectSegment> commandEffects;
  final String jsonContent;
  final String summaryMarkdown;
  final Uint8List mapPngBytes;
  final int commandCount;
  final int scanCount;
  final double mappedAreaM2;
  final double coverage;
  final bool hasMp4Video;

  const RunBundle({
    required this.runId,
    required this.mode,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.endedAt,
    required this.csvContent,
    required this.commandEffectsCsvContent,
    required this.commandEffects,
    required this.jsonContent,
    required this.summaryMarkdown,
    required this.mapPngBytes,
    required this.commandCount,
    required this.scanCount,
    required this.mappedAreaM2,
    required this.coverage,
    this.hasMp4Video = false,
  });

  /// MethodChannel 経由で Kotlin 側へ渡すファイルマップ。
  Map<String, Object> toChannelFiles() => {
    'driving_log_$runId.csv': csvContent,
    'command_effects_$runId.csv': commandEffectsCsvContent,
    'floor_map_$runId.png': mapPngBytes,
    'floor_map_$runId.json': jsonContent,
    'run_summary_$runId.md': summaryMarkdown,
  };
}

class _OpenCommandSegment {
  final int index;
  final int startElapsedMs;
  final int drivePwm;
  final int steerPwm;
  final Pose? startPose;
  final double? startFrontMinM;

  _OpenCommandSegment({
    required this.index,
    required this.startElapsedMs,
    required this.drivePwm,
    required this.steerPwm,
    required this.startPose,
    required this.startFrontMinM,
  });
}

/// 1回の走行中のコマンド・深度スキャン・軌跡を記録し、[RunBundle] を組み立てる。
class RunRecorder {
  static const int maxRows = 20000;
  static const int minScanIntervalMs = 100;

  final String runId;
  final String mode;
  final DateTime startedAt;

  final StringBuffer _csv = StringBuffer();
  final List<TrajectoryPoint> _trajectory = [];
  final List<CommandEffectSegment> _segments = [];
  _OpenCommandSegment? _openSegment;
  Pose? _latestKnownPose;
  double? _latestKnownFrontMinM;

  int _commandCount = 0;
  int _scanCount = 0;
  int _lastDrivePwm = 0;
  int _lastSteerPwm = 0;
  int _lastScanRecordedMs = -minScanIntervalMs;
  int _lastTrajectoryMs = -1000;
  double? _lastTrajectoryX;
  double? _lastTrajectoryY;

  RunRecorder({String? runId, required this.mode, DateTime? startedAt})
    : startedAt = startedAt ?? DateTime.now(),
      runId = runId ?? formatRunId(startedAt ?? DateTime.now()) {
    _csv.writeln(
      'timestamp_ms,elapsed_ms,event,mode,drive_pwm,steer_pwm,'
      'x_m,y_m,heading_rad,tracking_state,min_depth_m,rays_m',
    );
  }

  static String formatRunId(DateTime dt) {
    String d2(int v) => v.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    return '${dt.year}${d2(dt.month)}${d2(dt.day)}_'
        '${d2(dt.hour)}${d2(dt.minute)}${d2(dt.second)}';
  }

  int get rowCount => _commandCount + _scanCount;
  int get commandCount => _commandCount;
  int get scanCount => _scanCount;
  List<TrajectoryPoint> get trajectory => List.unmodifiable(_trajectory);
  List<CommandEffectSegment> get commandEffects => List.unmodifiable(_segments);

  static const double carCorridorHalfWidthM = 0.12; // 実車幅11cm + 左右余裕6.5cm (幅24cm)

  static double? frontMinDepthM(Scan? scan) {
    if (scan == null || scan.ranges.isEmpty) return null;
    final n = scan.ranges.length;
    final halfFov = math.pi / 6; // ±30°
    final vals = <double>[];
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      final r = scan.ranges[i];
      if (!r.isFinite || r <= 0) continue;
      final theta = n == 1 ? 0.0 : -halfFov + (2 * halfFov) * i / (n - 1);
      final lateralM = (r * math.sin(theta)).abs();
      // 車幅24cmの進路コリドー内に入る障害物だけを抽出する
      if (lateralM <= carCorridorHalfWidthM) {
        vals.add(r * math.cos(theta));
      }
    }
    if (vals.isEmpty) return null;
    vals.sort();
    // 1本だけの反射スパイクや床ノイズによる誤停止を防ぐため、2番目に近い値を採用する
    return vals.length >= 2 ? vals[1] : vals[0];
  }

  static double _wrapDegDiff(double radDiff) {
    var r = radDiff % (2 * math.pi);
    if (r > math.pi) r -= 2 * math.pi;
    if (r <= -math.pi) r += 2 * math.pi;
    return r * 180.0 / math.pi;
  }

  static double _wrapDeg(double rad) => _wrapDegDiff(rad);

  void _closeOpenSegment(
    int endElapsedMs,
    Pose? endPose,
    double? endFrontMinM,
  ) {
    final seg = _openSegment;
    if (seg == null) return;
    _openSegment = null;
    final sp = seg.startPose;
    final ep = endPose ?? sp;
    final sf = seg.startFrontMinM;
    final ef = endFrontMinM ?? sf;

    final double? dxM = (sp != null && ep != null) ? ep.x - sp.x : null;
    final double? dyM = (sp != null && ep != null) ? ep.y - sp.y : null;
    final double? distM = (dxM != null && dyM != null)
        ? math.sqrt(dxM * dxM + dyM * dyM)
        : null;
    final double? dHeadingDeg = (sp != null && ep != null)
        ? _wrapDegDiff(ep.heading - sp.heading)
        : null;
    final double? dFrontM = (sf != null && ef != null) ? ef - sf : null;

    _segments.add(
      CommandEffectSegment(
        index: seg.index,
        startElapsedMs: seg.startElapsedMs,
        endElapsedMs: math.max(seg.startElapsedMs, endElapsedMs),
        drivePwm: seg.drivePwm,
        steerPwm: seg.steerPwm,
        actionLabel: classifyCommandAction(seg.drivePwm, seg.steerPwm),
        startX: sp?.x,
        startY: sp?.y,
        startHeadingDeg: sp == null ? null : _wrapDeg(sp.heading),
        endX: ep?.x,
        endY: ep?.y,
        endHeadingDeg: ep == null ? null : _wrapDeg(ep.heading),
        dxM: dxM,
        dyM: dyM,
        distanceMovedM: distM,
        dHeadingDeg: dHeadingDeg,
        startFrontMinM: sf,
        endFrontMinM: ef,
        dFrontMinM: dFrontM,
      ),
    );
  }

  void recordCommand(
    int drivePwm,
    int steerPwm, {
    Pose? pose,
    String trackingState = '',
    Scan? latestScan,
    DateTime? at,
  }) {
    _lastDrivePwm = drivePwm;
    _lastSteerPwm = steerPwm;

    final now = at ?? DateTime.now();
    final elapsedMs = now.difference(startedAt).inMilliseconds;
    final effPose = pose ?? latestScan?.pose ?? _latestKnownPose;
    final effFrontMin = frontMinDepthM(latestScan) ?? _latestKnownFrontMinM;
    if (effPose != null) _latestKnownPose = effPose;
    if (effFrontMin != null) _latestKnownFrontMinM = effFrontMin;

    final open = _openSegment;
    if (open == null ||
        open.drivePwm != drivePwm ||
        open.steerPwm != steerPwm) {
      if (open != null) {
        _closeOpenSegment(elapsedMs, effPose, effFrontMin);
      }
      _openSegment = _OpenCommandSegment(
        index: _segments.length + 1,
        startElapsedMs: elapsedMs,
        drivePwm: drivePwm,
        steerPwm: steerPwm,
        startPose: effPose,
        startFrontMinM: effFrontMin,
      );
    }

    if (rowCount >= maxRows) return;

    _appendRow(
      timestampMs: now.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      elapsedMs: elapsedMs,
      event: 'cmd',
      drivePwm: drivePwm,
      steerPwm: steerPwm,
      pose: pose ?? latestScan?.pose,
      trackingState: trackingState,
      scan: latestScan,
    );
    _commandCount++;
    if (pose != null) {
      _maybeAddTrajectoryPoint(elapsedMs, pose);
    }
  }

  void recordScan(Scan scan, {required String trackingState, DateTime? at}) {
    final now = at ?? DateTime.now();
    final elapsedMs = now.difference(startedAt).inMilliseconds;
    _latestKnownPose = scan.pose;
    final fMin = frontMinDepthM(scan);
    if (fMin != null) _latestKnownFrontMinM = fMin;
    _maybeAddTrajectoryPoint(elapsedMs, scan.pose);

    if (rowCount >= maxRows) return;
    if (elapsedMs - _lastScanRecordedMs < minScanIntervalMs) return;
    _lastScanRecordedMs = elapsedMs;

    _appendRow(
      timestampMs: now.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      elapsedMs: elapsedMs,
      event: 'scan',
      drivePwm: _lastDrivePwm,
      steerPwm: _lastSteerPwm,
      pose: scan.pose,
      trackingState: trackingState,
      scan: scan,
    );
    _scanCount++;
  }

  void _maybeAddTrajectoryPoint(int elapsedMs, Pose pose) {
    final lx = _lastTrajectoryX;
    final ly = _lastTrajectoryY;
    if (lx == null || ly == null) {
      _trajectory.add((
        elapsedMs: elapsedMs,
        x: pose.x,
        y: pose.y,
        heading: pose.heading,
      ));
      _lastTrajectoryX = pose.x;
      _lastTrajectoryY = pose.y;
      _lastTrajectoryMs = elapsedMs;
      return;
    }
    final distSq =
        (pose.x - lx) * (pose.x - lx) + (pose.y - ly) * (pose.y - ly);
    if (distSq >= 0.03 * 0.03 || elapsedMs - _lastTrajectoryMs >= 500) {
      _trajectory.add((
        elapsedMs: elapsedMs,
        x: pose.x,
        y: pose.y,
        heading: pose.heading,
      ));
      _lastTrajectoryX = pose.x;
      _lastTrajectoryY = pose.y;
      _lastTrajectoryMs = elapsedMs;
    }
  }

  void _appendRow({
    required int timestampMs,
    required int elapsedMs,
    required String event,
    required int drivePwm,
    required int steerPwm,
    required Pose? pose,
    required String trackingState,
    required Scan? scan,
  }) {
    final xStr = pose == null ? '' : pose.x.toStringAsFixed(4);
    final yStr = pose == null ? '' : pose.y.toStringAsFixed(4);
    final hStr = pose == null ? '' : pose.heading.toStringAsFixed(4);

    var minDepthStr = '';
    var raysStr = '';
    if (scan != null && scan.ranges.isNotEmpty) {
      var minVal = double.infinity;
      final rayTokens = <String>[];
      for (final r in scan.ranges) {
        if (r.isFinite && r > 0) {
          if (r < minVal) minVal = r;
          rayTokens.add(r.toStringAsFixed(3));
        } else {
          rayTokens.add('inf');
        }
      }
      if (minVal.isFinite) {
        minDepthStr = minVal.toStringAsFixed(3);
      }
      raysStr = rayTokens.join(';');
    }

    _csv.writeln(
      '$timestampMs,$elapsedMs,${_csvCell(event)},${_csvCell(mode)},'
      '$drivePwm,$steerPwm,$xStr,$yStr,$hStr,${_csvCell(trackingState)},'
      '$minDepthStr,${_csvCell(raysStr)}',
    );
  }

  static String _csvCell(String raw) {
    if (!raw.contains(',') && !raw.contains('"') && !raw.contains('\n')) {
      return raw;
    }
    final escaped = raw.replaceAll('"', '""');
    return '"$escaped"';
  }

  static String _fmtOpt(double? v, int digits) =>
      v == null ? '' : v.toStringAsFixed(digits);

  static String _buildCommandEffectsCsv(List<CommandEffectSegment> segments) {
    final buf = StringBuffer()
      ..writeln(
        'index,start_elapsed_ms,end_elapsed_ms,duration_ms,'
        'drive_pwm,steer_pwm,action_label,'
        'start_x_m,start_y_m,start_heading_deg,'
        'end_x_m,end_y_m,end_heading_deg,'
        'dx_m,dy_m,distance_moved_m,d_heading_deg,'
        'start_front_min_m,end_front_min_m,d_front_min_m',
      );
    for (final s in segments) {
      buf.writeln(
        '${s.index},${s.startElapsedMs},${s.endElapsedMs},${s.durationMs},'
        '${s.drivePwm},${s.steerPwm},${_csvCell(s.actionLabel)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.startX, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.startY, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.startHeadingDeg, 2)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.endX, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.endY, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.endHeadingDeg, 2)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.dxM, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.dyM, 4)},${_fmtOpt(s.distanceMovedM, 4)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.dHeadingDeg, 2)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.startFrontMinM, 3)},${_fmtOpt(s.endFrontMinM, 3)},'
        '${_fmtOpt(s.dFrontMinM, 3)}',
      );
    }
    return buf.toString();
  }

  RunBundle buildBundle({
    required OccupancyGrid grid,
    required MappingParams params,
    required double speedNorm,
    required int currentDrivePwm,
    required bool invertSteer,
    Cell? robotCell,
    Pose? latestPose,
    Scan? latestScan,
    bool hasMp4Video = false,
    DateTime? endedAt,
  }) {
    final end = endedAt ?? DateTime.now();
    final endElapsedMs = math.max(0, end.difference(startedAt).inMilliseconds);
    final closingPose = latestPose ?? latestScan?.pose ?? _latestKnownPose;
    final closingFrontMin = frontMinDepthM(latestScan) ?? _latestKnownFrontMinM;
    if (_openSegment != null) {
      _closeOpenSegment(endElapsedMs, closingPose, closingFrontMin);
    }
    final segments = List<CommandEffectSegment>.unmodifiable(_segments);
    final effectsCsv = _buildCommandEffectsCsv(segments);

    final durationSec = math.max(0.0, endElapsedMs / 1000.0);
    final mappedAreaM2 = grid.knownCount * params.cellSize * params.cellSize;
    final coverage = grid.coverage;

    final mapPngBytes = encodeOccupancyGridPng(
      grid: grid,
      trajectory: _trajectory,
      params: params,
      robotCell: robotCell,
    );

    final jsonPayload = {
      'runId': runId,
      'mode': mode,
      'startedAt': startedAt.toIso8601String(),
      'endedAt': end.toIso8601String(),
      'durationSec': double.parse(durationSec.toStringAsFixed(2)),
      'hasMp4Video': hasMp4Video,
      'mappingParams': {
        'cellSizeM': params.cellSize,
        'gridW': params.gridW,
        'gridH': params.gridH,
        'roomWM': params.roomW,
        'roomHM': params.roomH,
      },
      'settings': {
        'speedNorm': double.parse(speedNorm.toStringAsFixed(2)),
        'currentDrivePwm': currentDrivePwm,
        'invertSteer': invertSteer,
      },
      'stats': {
        'mappedAreaM2': double.parse(mappedAreaM2.toStringAsFixed(3)),
        'coverage': double.parse(coverage.toStringAsFixed(4)),
        'knownCount': grid.knownCount,
        'commandCount': _commandCount,
        'commandSegmentCount': segments.length,
        'scanCount': _scanCount,
      },
      'trajectory': [
        for (final pt in _trajectory)
          {
            'elapsedMs': pt.elapsedMs,
            'x': double.parse(pt.x.toStringAsFixed(4)),
            'y': double.parse(pt.y.toStringAsFixed(4)),
            'heading': double.parse(pt.heading.toStringAsFixed(4)),
          },
      ],
      'commandEffects': [for (final seg in segments) seg.toJson()],
      'logOdds': [
        for (final v in grid.logOdds) double.parse(v.toStringAsFixed(3)),
      ],
    };

    final md = StringBuffer()
      ..writeln('# 走行記録レポート ($runId)')
      ..writeln()
      ..writeln('- 走行ID: $runId')
      ..writeln('- 走行モード: $mode')
      ..writeln('- 開始時刻: ${startedAt.toIso8601String()}')
      ..writeln(
        '- 終了時刻: ${end.toIso8601String()}（${durationSec.toStringAsFixed(1)} 秒）',
      )
      ..writeln(
        '- 対象スケール: ${params.roomW.toStringAsFixed(1)} m × '
        '${params.roomH.toStringAsFixed(1)} m'
        '（グリッド ${params.gridW}×${params.gridH}、セル '
        '${(params.cellSize * 100).round()} cm）',
      )
      ..writeln(
        '- マッピング面積: ${mappedAreaM2.toStringAsFixed(2)} m²'
        '（カバー率 ${(coverage * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%）',
      )
      ..writeln(
        '- 記録件数: コマンド $_commandCount 件（命令区間 ${segments.length} 件） / '
        '深度スキャン $_scanCount 件 / 軌跡 ${_trajectory.length} 点',
      )
      ..writeln(
        '- 走行設定: 速度 ${(speedNorm * 100).round()}% '
        '(PWM $currentDrivePwm), 操舵反転 ${invertSteer ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}',
      )
      ..writeln(
        '- カメラMP4録画: ${hasMp4Video ? 'camera_recording_$runId.mp4 保存済み' : 'なし'}',
      )
      ..writeln()
      ..writeln('## 出力ファイル')
      ..writeln('- `driving_log_$runId.csv`: 時系列操作コマンド・ARCore自己位置・21本深度レイ')
      ..writeln('- `command_effects_$runId.csv`: 命令送信ごとの位置・向き・前方距離の変化一覧')
      ..writeln('- `floor_map_$runId.png`: 占有格子地図と走行軌跡画像')
      ..writeln('- `floor_map_$runId.json`: グリッド数値データ・軌跡・命令効果JSON');

    if (hasMp4Video) {
      md.writeln('- `camera_recording_$runId.mp4`: ARCore カメラ映像＋センサー動画');
    }
    if (segments.isNotEmpty) {
      md
        ..writeln()
        ..writeln('## コマンド別の車体・センサー変化一覧（命令→結果）')
        ..writeln()
        ..writeln(
          '| # | 時間 [ms] | 操作 | PWM (駆動,操舵) | 移動距離 [m] | 向き変化 [°] | 前方距離変化 [m] |',
        )
        ..writeln('|---:|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---:|');
      for (final s in segments) {
        final mv = s.distanceMovedM == null
            ? '—'
            : s.distanceMovedM!.toStringAsFixed(2);
        final dh = s.dHeadingDeg == null
            ? '—'
            : s.dHeadingDeg!.toStringAsFixed(1);
        final df = s.dFrontMinM == null
            ? '—'
            : s.dFrontMinM!.toStringAsFixed(2);
        md.writeln(
          '| ${s.index} | ${s.durationMs} | ${s.actionLabel} | '
          '(${s.drivePwm}, ${s.steerPwm}) | $mv | $dh | $df |',
        );
      }
    }

    return RunBundle(
      runId: runId,
      mode: mode,
      startedAt: startedAt,
      endedAt: end,
      csvContent: _csv.toString(),
      commandEffectsCsvContent: effectsCsv,
      commandEffects: segments,
      jsonContent: jsonEncode(jsonPayload),
      summaryMarkdown: md.toString(),
      mapPngBytes: mapPngBytes,
      commandCount: _commandCount,
      scanCount: _scanCount,
      mappedAreaM2: mappedAreaM2,
      coverage: coverage,
      hasMp4Video: hasMp4Video,
    );
  }
}

/// 占有格子地図と走行軌跡を RGB PNG 画像（外部ライブラリなし・純 Dart）として書き出す。
Uint8List encodeOccupancyGridPng({
  required OccupancyGrid grid,
  required List<TrajectoryPoint> trajectory,
  required MappingParams params,
  Cell? robotCell,
}) {
  final scale = params.gridW <= 120 ? 4 : 2;
  final width = params.gridW * scale;
  final height = params.gridH * scale;
  final pixels = Uint8List(width * height * 3);

  void fillCell(int r, int c, int rr, int gg, int bb) {
    if (r < 0 || r >= params.gridH || c < 0 || c >= params.gridW) return;
    // MapPainter と同様に北（y 増加方向）を画像の上へ配置する。
    final imgY0 = (params.gridH - 1 - r) * scale;
    final imgX0 = c * scale;
    for (var dy = 0; dy < scale; dy++) {
      final rowOffset = ((imgY0 + dy) * width + imgX0) * 3;
      for (var dx = 0; dx < scale; dx++) {
        final idx = rowOffset + dx * 3;
        pixels[idx] = rr;
        pixels[idx + 1] = gg;
        pixels[idx + 2] = bb;
      }
    }
  }

  for (var r = 0; r < params.gridH; r++) {
    for (var c = 0; c < params.gridW; c++) {
      final v = grid.logOdds[r * params.gridW + c];
      if (v.abs() <= 1e-6) {
        fillCell(r, c, 229, 231, 235);
      } else if (v < 0) {
        fillCell(r, c, 255, 255, 255);
      } else {
        fillCell(r, c, 17, 24, 39);
      }
    }
  }

  if (trajectory.isNotEmpty) {
    Cell? prev;
    for (final pt in trajectory) {
      final cur = cellOf(params, pt.x, pt.y);
      if (prev != null) {
        final steps = math.max((cur.r - prev.r).abs(), (cur.c - prev.c).abs());
        if (steps > 1) {
          for (var s = 1; s < steps; s++) {
            final t = s / steps;
            final ir = (prev.r + (cur.r - prev.r) * t).round();
            final ic = (prev.c + (cur.c - prev.c) * t).round();
            fillCell(ir, ic, 37, 99, 235);
          }
        }
      }
      fillCell(cur.r, cur.c, 37, 99, 235);
      prev = cur;
    }

    final startCell = cellOf(params, trajectory.first.x, trajectory.first.y);
    for (var dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) {
      for (var dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
        fillCell(startCell.r + dr, startCell.c + dc, 22, 163, 74);
      }
    }
  }

  final endCell =
      robotCell ??
      (trajectory.isNotEmpty
          ? cellOf(params, trajectory.last.x, trajectory.last.y)
          : null);
  if (endCell != null) {
    for (var dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) {
      for (var dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
        fillCell(endCell.r + dr, endCell.c + dc, 220, 38, 38);
      }
    }
  }

  return _encodeRgbPng(width, height, pixels);
}

Uint8List _encodeRgbPng(int width, int height, Uint8List rgbPixels) {
  final stride = width * 3;
  final raw = Uint8List(height * (1 + stride));
  for (var y = 0; y < height; y++) {
    final dstRow = y * (1 + stride);
    raw[dstRow] = 0; // filter type None
    final srcRow = y * stride;
    raw.setRange(dstRow + 1, dstRow + 1 + stride, rgbPixels, srcRow);
  }
  final compressed = Uint8List.fromList(zlib.encode(raw));

  final ihdr = ByteData(13)
    ..setUint32(0, width, Endian.big)
    ..setUint32(4, height, Endian.big)
    ..setUint8(8, 8) // bit depth
    ..setUint8(9, 2) // color type RGB
    ..setUint8(10, 0) // compression
    ..setUint8(11, 0) // filter
    ..setUint8(12, 0); // interlace

  final out = BytesBuilder(copy: false)
    ..add(const [0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A])
    ..add(_pngChunk('IHDR', ihdr.buffer.asUint8List()))
    ..add(_pngChunk('IDAT', compressed))
    ..add(_pngChunk('IEND', Uint8List(0)));
  return out.takeBytes();
}

Uint8List _pngChunk(String type, Uint8List data) {
  final typeBytes = ascii.encode(type);
  final header = ByteData(4)..setUint32(0, data.length, Endian.big);

  final crcInput = Uint8List(4 + data.length)
    ..setRange(0, 4, typeBytes)
    ..setRange(4, 4 + data.length, data);
  final crc = ByteData(4)..setUint32(0, _crc32(crcInput), Endian.big);

  return (BytesBuilder(copy: false)
        ..add(header.buffer.asUint8List())
        ..add(crcInput)
        ..add(crc.buffer.asUint8List()))
      .takeBytes();
}

final List<int> _crcTable = List<int>.generate(256, (n) {
  var c = n;
  for (var k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
    c = (c & 1) != 0 ? (0xEDB88320 ^ (c >>> 1)) : (c >>> 1);
  }
  return c;
});

int _crc32(Uint8List bytes) {
  var c = 0xFFFFFFFF;
  for (final b in bytes) {
    c = _crcTable[(c ^ b) & 0xFF] ^ (c >>> 8);
  }
  return (c ^ 0xFFFFFFFF) & 0xFFFFFFFF;
}

/// 走行記録保存・Google ドライブ連携の保存結果。
class SaveRunResult {
  final String savedDir;
  final String driveAction;
  final bool ok;

  const SaveRunResult({
    required this.savedDir,
    required this.driveAction,
    required this.ok,
  });
}

/// 走行記録を端末領域および Google ドライブへ保存するストレージ抽象。
abstract class RunStorage {
  Future<SaveRunResult> saveRunBundle(
    RunBundle bundle, {
    required bool autoDriveSync,
    required bool openDriveSheet,
  });

  Future<bool> shareRunToDrive({String? runId});

  Future<String> getWebhookUrl();

  Future<bool> setWebhookUrl(String url);
}

/// Android ネイティブ実装（[LogStorageManager]）を呼び出す MethodChannel ラッパ。
class MethodChannelRunStorage implements RunStorage {
  static const MethodChannel _channel = MethodChannel('phone_robot/storage');

  @override
  Future<SaveRunResult> saveRunBundle(
    RunBundle bundle, {
    required bool autoDriveSync,
    required bool openDriveSheet,
  }) async {
    try {
      final raw = await _channel
          .invokeMethod<Map<dynamic, dynamic>>('saveRunBundle', {
            'runId': bundle.runId,
            'files': bundle.toChannelFiles(),
            'autoDriveSync': autoDriveSync,
            'openDriveSheet': openDriveSheet,
          });
      if (raw == null) {
        return const SaveRunResult(
          savedDir: '',
          driveAction: 'error',
          ok: false,
        );
      }
      return SaveRunResult(
        savedDir: raw['savedDir']?.toString() ?? '',
        driveAction: raw['driveAction']?.toString() ?? 'local_only',
        ok: raw['ok'] == true,
      );
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return const SaveRunResult(
        savedDir: '',
        driveAction: 'unavailable',
        ok: false,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> shareRunToDrive({String? runId}) async {
    try {
      final ok = await _channel.invokeMethod<bool>('shareRunToDrive', {
        'runId': runId,
      });
      return ok ?? false;
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return false;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<String> getWebhookUrl() async {
    try {
      final url = await _channel.invokeMethod<String>('getWebhookUrl');
      return url ?? '';
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return '';
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> setWebhookUrl(String url) async {
    try {
      final ok = await _channel.invokeMethod<bool>('setWebhookUrl', {
        'url': url,
      });
      return ok ?? false;
    } on MissingPluginException {
      return false;
    }
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

/// ESP32 との USB シリアルリンクの状態。
enum Esp32LinkState { disconnected, connecting, connected, error }

/// USB シリアルデバイスの情報（ネイティブ側の列挙結果）。
class Esp32Device {
  final int deviceId;
  final int vendorId;
  final int productId;
  final String name;

  const Esp32Device({
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.vendorId,
    required this.productId,
    required this.name,
  });

  factory Esp32Device.fromMap(Map<dynamic, dynamic> m) => Esp32Device(
        deviceId: (m['deviceId'] as num).toInt(),
        vendorId: (m['vendorId'] as num).toInt(),
        productId: (m['productId'] as num).toInt(),
        name: m['name']?.toString() ?? 'unknown',
      );

  @override
  String toString() =>
      '$name (VID ${vendorId.toRadixString(16)} / PID ${productId.toRadixString(16)})';
}

/// ESP32 へのシリアルリンク（ファーム v2.0 系プロトコル）。
///
/// プロトコル: 115200 bps、コマンド "c,drive,steer\n"（連続値）と
/// 単文字コマンド f/b/l/r/s。ファーム側に 400 ms の通信断ウォッチ
/// ドッグがあるため、走行中は 5 Hz 以上でコマンドを送り続ける必要が
/// ある（RealDriver の制御ループが 10 Hz で送る）。停止は 's' を送る
/// か、送信を止めればウォッチドッグで止まる。
abstract class Esp32Link {
  /// リンク状態の変化。
  Stream<Esp32LinkState> get states;

  /// 現在のリンク状態（最後に分かった値）。
  Esp32LinkState get state;

  /// ESP32 から受信したテキスト（ログ表示用）。
  Stream<String> get received;

  /// 最後に送信したコマンド文字列（UI の「現在の指令」表示用）。
  String get lastCommand;

  /// 駆動・操舵指令を送信した直後に呼ばれるコールバック（走行ログ記録用）。
  void Function(int drive, int steer)? onCommandSent;

  Future<List<Esp32Device>> listDevices();

  /// 接続する。[deviceId] が null なら先頭の対応デバイスを使う。
  /// USB 権限が未許可ならネイティブ側が権限要求ダイアログを出す。
  Future<bool> connect({int? deviceId, int baudRate = 115200});

  Future<void> disconnect();

  /// 生テキストを送る。
  Future<bool> send(String text);

  /// 連続値コマンド "c,drive,steer\n" を送る。
  /// drive / steer の値域はファーム側の受付範囲に合わせる
  /// （[SerialCommandScale] を参照）。
  Future<bool> sendDriveSteer(int drive, int steer);

  /// 単文字コマンド（f/b/l/r/s）を送る。手動ジョグ用。
  Future<bool> sendJog(String ch);
}

/// drive / steer の整数レンジ変換。
///
/// ファーム（esp32_toyota86 / esp32_toyota86_asbuilt）の "c,drive,steer" は
/// -255..255 の PWM duty を受け付ける。駆動モーターは静止摩擦デッドゾーンを
/// 超える [minEffectiveDrivePwm..maxValue] へ [driveOf] でマッピングする。
class SerialCommandScale {
  static const int maxValue = 255;
  static const int minEffectiveDrivePwm = 140;

  static int of(double normalized) {
    final v = (normalized.clamp(-1.0, 1.0) * maxValue).round();
    return v;
  }

  /// 駆動モーター用: 0 は 0、非ゼロはギアボックスの静止摩擦を超える
  /// [minEffectiveDrivePwm..maxValue] に線形マッピングする。
  static int driveOf(double normalized) {
    final clamped = normalized.clamp(-1.0, 1.0);
    final mag = clamped.abs();
    if (mag < 1e-3) return 0;
    final pwm = (minEffectiveDrivePwm +
            mag * (maxValue - minEffectiveDrivePwm))
        .round()
        .clamp(minEffectiveDrivePwm, maxValue);
    return clamped < 0 ? -pwm : pwm;
  }
}

/// プラットフォームチャネル経由の実装（ネイティブ側は
/// usb-serial-for-android を使う SerialManager.kt）。
class MethodChannelEsp32Link implements Esp32Link {
  static const MethodChannel _method = MethodChannel('phone_robot/serial');
  static const EventChannel _stateEvents =
      EventChannel('phone_robot/serial/state');
  static const EventChannel _rxEvents = EventChannel('phone_robot/serial/rx');

  Esp32LinkState _state = Esp32LinkState.disconnected;
  String _lastCommand = '(なし)';
  Stream<Esp32LinkState>? _states;

  @override
  void Function(int drive, int steer)? onCommandSent;

  @override
  Esp32LinkState get state => _state;

  @override
  String get lastCommand => _lastCommand;

  @override
  Stream<Esp32LinkState> get states {
    return _states ??= _stateEvents.receiveBroadcastStream().map((dynamic e) {
      final s = switch (e.toString()) {
        'connected' => Esp32LinkState.connected,
        'connecting' => Esp32LinkState.connecting,
        'error' => Esp32LinkState.error,
        _ => Esp32LinkState.disconnected,
      };
      _state = s;
      return s;
    });
  }

  @override
  Stream<String> get received =>
      _rxEvents.receiveBroadcastStream().map((dynamic e) => e.toString());

  @override
  Future<List<Esp32Device>> listDevices() async {
    final raw = await _method.invokeMethod<List<dynamic>>('listDevices');
    if (raw == null) return const [];
    return raw
        .map((e) => Esp32Device.fromMap(e as Map<dynamic, dynamic>))
        .toList();
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> connect({int? deviceId, int baudRate = 115200}) async {
    final ok = await _method.invokeMethod<bool>('connect', {
      'deviceId': deviceId,
      'baudRate': baudRate,
    });
    if (ok == true) {
      _state = Esp32LinkState.connected;
    }
    return ok ?? false;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> disconnect() async {
    await _method.invokeMethod<void>('disconnect');
    _state = Esp32LinkState.disconnected;
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> send(String text) async {
    _lastCommand = text.replaceAll('\n', r'\n');
    final ok = await _method.invokeMethod<bool>('send', {'text': text});
    return ok ?? false;
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> sendDriveSteer(int drive, int steer) async {
    final ok = await send('c,$drive,$steer\n');
    onCommandSent?.call(drive, steer);
    return ok;
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> sendJog(String ch) async {
    final ok = await send(ch);
    if (ch == 's') {
      onCommandSent?.call(0, 0);
    }
    return ok;
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:phone_robot_core/phone_robot_core.dart';

import '../platform/serial_link.dart';

/// 深度スキャンの受け渡し口。RealSession が DepthSource のストリームを
/// ここへ流し込み、RealDriver が「新しいスキャンが来たら地図へ登録」
/// というタイミング制御を行う。
class ScanFeed {
  Scan? latest;
  int seq = 0;
  DateTime? lastAt;

  void add(Scan s) {
    latest = s;
    seq++;
    lastAt = DateTime.now();
  }

  bool get isFresh =>
      lastAt != null &&
      DateTime.now().difference(lastAt!) < const Duration(milliseconds: 600);
}

/// ポーズの受け渡し口（ScanFeed と対）。RealSession が PoseSource の
/// ストリームをここへ流し込む。ポーズ本体に加えて「いつ届いたか」と
/// 「ARCore のトラッキング状態」を保持し、RealDriver の鮮度ゲート
/// （R1: 古いポーズ・トラッキング喪失では進まない）の入力になる。
class PoseFeed {
  Pose? latest;
  String trackingState = '未開始';
  DateTime? lastPoseAt;

  void addPose(Pose p) {
    latest = p;
    lastPoseAt = DateTime.now();
  }

  void setTrackingState(String s) {
    trackingState = s;
  }

  bool get isTracking => trackingState == 'TRACKING';

  /// 最新ポーズが [maxAge] 以内に届いていれば true。
  bool poseFresh(Duration maxAge) {
    final t = lastPoseAt;
    if (t == null || latest == null) return false;
    return DateTime.now().difference(t) <= maxAge;
  }
}

/// ウェイポイント走行の結果。
///
/// - [blocked] / [timeout]: 指令は届いていたが進めなかった（障害物の疑い）。
/// - [replan]: センサー喪失から復帰した。再計画して続行できる。
/// - [sensorLost]: センサーが復帰しなかった（[RealDriver.lastStopReason]
///   に 'tracking-lost' / 'scan-stale'）。地図へ障害物は書いていない。
/// - [linkLost]: ESP32 リンク切断または送信失敗（R3）。この場合も
///   地図へ障害物は書いていない。
enum DriveOutcome {
  arrived,
  blocked,
  timeout,
  aborted,
  replan,
  sensorLost,
  linkLost,
}

/// 実機ドライバ。
///
/// 中核（phone_robot_core）の MotionHooks は同期的だが、プラットフォーム
/// チャネル（ARCore / USB シリアル）は非同期にしか扱えない。そのため
/// 実機では、Explorer / GoalNavigator と同じ手順・同じガードを保ったまま、
/// 非同期の駆動ループとしてここに実装する。計画そのものは中核の部品
/// （OccupancyGrid.planMask / frontierCells / findPath）を共用するので、
/// 地図と計画の意味はシミュレーションと同一になる。
///
/// 経路（セル列）は方向転換点と最大間隔でウェイポイント列に圧縮し、
/// ARCore ポーズを見ながら連続制御で辿る（SPEC §5.3）。
///
/// 安全ゲート（NOTE10PLUS_RISKS R1〜R3）: 駆動の各周期で
/// (1) リンクが接続されていること、(2) トラッキングが TRACKING で
/// ポーズが新鮮なこと、(3) スキャンが新鮮なこと、を確認してからでないと
/// 駆動指令を出さない。喪失した場合は停止して短時間の復帰を待ち、
/// 復帰すれば再計画、しなければ理由付きで走行全体を止める。
/// 近接フェイルセーフ（新鮮なスキャンの最小距離が [proximityStopM] を
/// 切ったら即停止し、進行方向のセルを占有として記録して再計画）は
/// フェイルクローズ側に倒してある: スキャンが古ければ「障害物なし」
/// ではなく「進めない」になる。
class RealDriver {
  final MappingParams params;
  final OccupancyGrid grid;
  final Esp32Link link;
  final ScanFeed feed;

  /// believed pose の供給元（RealSession が PoseSource を流し込む）。
  final PoseFeed poseFeed;

  /// 状態メッセージの通知先（UI 表示用）。
  void Function(String status)? onStatus;

  /// 近接フェイルセーフの停止距離 [m]。
  /// スマホ（車体中心付近）から車体前面まで約 0.13 m + 余裕。
  double proximityStopM = 0.22;

  /// 通常走行の drive 正規化値（0..1）。
  double cruiseDrive = 0.6;

  /// ポーズの鮮度ゲート: これより古いポーズでは進まない（R1）。
  Duration poseMaxAge = const Duration(milliseconds: 500);

  /// トラッキング喪失時に復帰を待つ上限。これを超えたら諦めて止める。
  Duration trackingRecoveryTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 5);

  /// スキャンが古くなったときに新しいスキャンを待つ上限（R2）。
  Duration scanRecoveryTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 2);

  /// ウェイポイント1つあたりの走行タイムアウト。
  Duration waypointTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 30);

  /// sensorLost で止まった理由（'tracking-lost' / 'scan-stale' / 'link-lost'）。
  String? lastStopReason;

  /// HUD 表示用: 現在の目標進行方位 [rad] と前方自由距離 [m]。
  double? hudTargetHeading;
  double? hudTargetDistM;

  /// rotateTo で前方接近時に切り返し（K-turn）を行うか。
  bool enableKTurn = true;

  /// ステアリング出力の左右極性を反転するか（実車モーター配線合わせ用）。
  bool invertSteer = false;

  bool _aborted = false;
  bool _busy = false;
  int _appliedSeq = 0;
  double _lastScanX = 0;
  double _lastScanY = 0;
  double _lastScanHeading = 0;

  RealDriver({
    required this.params,
    required this.grid,
    required this.link,
    required this.feed,
    required this.poseFeed,
  });

  bool get isBusy => _busy;

  Pose get _pose {
    final p = poseFeed.latest;
    if (p == null) {
      throw StateError('ARCore のポーズがまだ得られていません');
    }
    return p;
  }

  /// ポーズが「信じて進める」状態か（TRACKING かつ新鮮）。
  bool get _poseOk => poseFeed.isTracking && poseFeed.poseFresh(poseMaxAge);

  Cell get currentCell {
    final p = _pose;
    final c = cellOf(params, p.x, p.y);
    return (
      r: c.r.clamp(0, params.gridH - 1),
      c: c.c.clamp(0, params.gridW - 1),
    );
  }

  double get heading => poseFeed.latest?.heading ?? _lastScanHeading;

  void _status(String s) => onStatus?.call(s);

  static double _normAngle(double a) {
    var r = a % (2 * math.pi);
    if (r > math.pi) r -= 2 * math.pi;
    if (r < -math.pi) r += 2 * math.pi;
    return r;
  }

  /// 停止を要求する。実行中のループは次の周期で止まり、's' を送る。
  Future<void> stop() async {
    _aborted = true;
    await link.sendJog('s');
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 安全ゲート（R1 / R2 / R3）
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  /// リンク確認（R3）。接続されていれば null、切断なら linkLost。
  DriveOutcome? _checkLink() {
    if (link.state == Esp32LinkState.connected) return null;
    lastStopReason = 'link-lost';
    _status('ESP32 リンクの切断を検知しました。停止します');
    return DriveOutcome.linkLost;
  }

  /// ポーズの健全性ゲート（R1）。
  ///
  /// 健全なら null（そのまま進む）。喪失していた場合は停止して
  /// [trackingRecoveryTimeout] まで復帰を待ち、復帰したら replan
  /// （外側で再計画）、しなければ sensorLost を返す。
  Future<DriveOutcome?> _gatePose() async {
    if (_poseOk) return null;
    await link.sendJog('s');
    _status('トラッキング喪失を検知: 停止して復帰を待ちます');
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    while (sw.elapsed < trackingRecoveryTimeout) {
      if (_aborted) return DriveOutcome.aborted;
      if (link.state != Esp32LinkState.connected) {
        return DriveOutcome.linkLost;
      }
      if (_poseOk) {
        _status('トラッキングが復帰しました。再計画します');
        return DriveOutcome.replan;
      }
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
    }
    lastStopReason = 'tracking-lost';
    _status('トラッキングが復帰しませんでした。走行を止めます');
    return DriveOutcome.sensorLost;
  }

  /// スキャンの鮮度ゲート（R2、フェイルクローズ）。
  ///
  /// 新鮮なら null（そのまま進む）。古ければ停止して
  /// [scanRecoveryTimeout] まで新しいスキャンを待ち、来れば続行、
  /// 来なければ sensorLost を返す。「データなし＝障害物なし」にはしない。
  Future<DriveOutcome?> _gateScan() async {
    if (feed.isFresh) return null;
    await link.sendJog('s');
    _status('深度スキャンが古いため停止: 新しいスキャンを待ちます');
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    while (sw.elapsed < scanRecoveryTimeout) {
      if (_aborted) return DriveOutcome.aborted;
      if (link.state != Esp32LinkState.connected) {
        return DriveOutcome.linkLost;
      }
      if (feed.isFresh) return null;
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
    }
    lastStopReason = 'scan-stale';
    _status('新しいスキャンが得られませんでした。走行を止めます');
    return DriveOutcome.sensorLost;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // スキャン登録
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  /// 新しいスキャンの到着を待ち、believed pose で地図へ登録する。
  /// タイムアウト時は登録せずに戻る（計測なしを異常扱いしない: SPEC §5.2）。
  /// ポーズが信じられない状態（トラッキング喪失・鮮度切れ）のスキャンは
  /// 位置がずれている疑いがあるため登録しない（R1）。
  Future<void> captureScan() async {
    final before = feed.seq;
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    while (feed.seq == before && sw.elapsed < const Duration(seconds: 2)) {
      if (_aborted) return;
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 30));
    }
    final s = feed.latest;
    if (s == null || feed.seq == _appliedSeq) return;
    if (!_poseOk) return;
    _appliedSeq = feed.seq;
    grid.applyScan(s);
    _lastScanX = s.pose.x;
    _lastScanY = s.pose.y;
    _lastScanHeading = s.pose.heading;
  }

  /// 走行中に「十分に動いたら」最新スキャンを登録する（間引き）。
  void applyScanIfMoved() {
    if (!_poseOk) return;
    final s = feed.latest;
    if (s == null || feed.seq == _appliedSeq) return;
    final dx = s.pose.x - _lastScanX;
    final dy = s.pose.y - _lastScanY;
    final moved = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    final turned = _normAngle(s.pose.heading - _lastScanHeading).abs();
    if (moved < 0.15 && turned < 0.30) return;
    _appliedSeq = feed.seq;
    grid.applyScan(s);
    _lastScanX = s.pose.x;
    _lastScanY = s.pose.y;
    _lastScanHeading = s.pose.heading;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 近接フェイルセーフ
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  /// 最新スキャンの最小距離 [m]。計測が無ければ null。
  /// 駆動中は _gateScan が鮮度を保証してから呼ばれる前提。
  double? get _minRange {
    final s = feed.latest;
    if (s == null || !feed.isFresh) return null;
    var m = double.infinity;
    for (final r in s.ranges) {
      if (r.isFinite && r < m) m = r;
    }
    return m.isFinite ? m : null;
  }

  bool get _proximityBlocked {
    final m = _minRange;
    return m != null && m < proximityStopM;
  }

  /// 進行方向のセルを強い占有証拠として記録する（SimRobot と同じ流儀）。
  ///
  /// これは「指令は届いたが進めなかった」場合専用（R3）。リンク切断・
  /// ポーズ不健全・スキャン不鮮のときは、進めなかった理由が障害物とは
  /// 限らないため記録しない（実在しない壁で地図を汚さない）。
  void _markBlockedAhead() {
    if (link.state != Esp32LinkState.connected) return;
    if (!_poseOk) return;
    if (!feed.isFresh) return;
    final p = _pose;
    final d = (_minRange ?? proximityStopM).clamp(0.10, 0.60);
    final bx = p.x + math.cos(p.heading) * d;
    final by = p.y + math.sin(p.heading) * d;
    final cell = cellOf(params, bx, by);
    if (!grid.inBounds(cell.r, cell.c)) return;
    final i = grid.index(cell.r, cell.c);
    if (grid.logOdds[i] < 2.0) grid.logOdds[i] = 2.0;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 基本動作（回転 / ウェイポイント走行 / パルス当て舵）
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  /// 送信結果をそのまま返す（R3: 送信失敗をドライバが見られるように）。
  /// 駆動はギアボックスの静止摩擦デッドゾーン補正済みの [driveOf] を使う。
  Future<bool> _sendNorm(double drive, double steer) {
    final s = invertSteer ? -steer : steer;
    return link.sendDriveSteer(
      SerialCommandScale.driveOf(drive),
      SerialCommandScale.of(s),
    );
  }

  /// Jozen 86 のバネ戻し付きバンバン操舵向け連続フルステア計算。
  /// 奇数ティックで 0 に落とすとバネで直進に戻ってしまうため、
  /// 不感帯（±0.09 rad ≈ 5°）の外では目標方向のフルステア（±1.0）を維持する。
  double _computePulseSteer(double err) {
    final absErr = err.abs();
    if (absErr < 0.09) {
      return 0.0;
    }
    return err > 0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
  }

  /// ゲートの結果を回転動作の可否に読み替える。
  /// null / replan（復帰した）なら続行、それ以外は失敗として止める。
  bool _gateBlocks(DriveOutcome? g) => g != null && g != DriveOutcome.replan;

  /// その場向き変え。車両はステアリング式のため、転がしながら ARCore の
  /// 向きが目標に近づくまで操舵する（SPEC §7 の読み替え）。
  /// 前方が詰まった場合は [enableKTurn] 有効時に最大2回まで切り返しを行う。
  Future<bool> rotateTo(
    double targetHeading, {
    Duration timeout = const Duration(seconds: 12),
  }) async {
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    var kTurns = 0;
    while (!_aborted && sw.elapsed < timeout) {
      if (_checkLink() != null) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return false;
      }
      if (_gateBlocks(await _gatePose())) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return false;
      }
      if (_gateBlocks(await _gateScan())) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return false;
      }
      final err = _normAngle(targetHeading - _pose.heading);
      if (err.abs() < 0.08) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return true;
      }
      final steer = err > 0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
      if (_proximityBlocked && err.abs() > 0.3) {
        if (!enableKTurn || kTurns >= 2) {
          await link.sendJog('s');
          return false;
        }
        kTurns++;
        _status('前方接近のため切り返し（K-turn $kTurns/2）…');
        // 急反転による USB 電圧降下防止のため一瞬停止を挟み、逆ステアで後退
        await link.sendJog('s');
        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 120));
        for (var step = 0; step < 3 && !_aborted; step++) {
          if (_checkLink() != null) {
            await link.sendJog('s');
            return false;
          }
          if (!await _sendNorm(-math.min(cruiseDrive, 0.55), -steer)) {
            lastStopReason = 'link-lost';
            _status('指令の送信に失敗しました（リンク切断の疑い）。停止します');
            await link.sendJog('s');
            return false;
          }
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
        }
        await link.sendJog('s');
        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 120));
        continue;
      }
      if (!await _sendNorm(math.min(cruiseDrive, 0.35), steer)) {
        lastStopReason = 'link-lost';
        _status('指令の送信に失敗しました（リンク切断の疑い）。停止します');
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return false;
      }
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
    }
    await link.sendJog('s');
    return false;
  }

  /// 360° 回転スキャン（30° × 12 方向）。中核の rotationScan と同じ。
  Future<void> rotationScan() async {
    final base = heading;
    for (var k = 0; k < 12; k++) {
      if (_aborted) return;
      await rotateTo(base + k * math.pi / 6);
      await captureScan();
    }
  }

  /// セル列をウェイポイント列に圧縮する（方向転換点＋最大 0.5 m 間隔）。
  List<Cell> _compressWaypoints(List<Cell> path) {
    if (path.length <= 2) return List.of(path);
    final out = <Cell>[path.first];
    var lastKept = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
      final dr1 = path[i].r - path[i - 1].r;
      final dc1 = path[i].c - path[i - 1].c;
      final dr2 = path[i + 1].r - path[i].r;
      final dc2 = path[i + 1].c - path[i].c;
      final turned = dr1 != dr2 || dc1 != dc2;
      final far = (i - lastKept) * params.cellSize >= 0.5;
      if (turned || far) {
        out.add(path[i]);
        lastKept = i;
      }
    }
    out.add(path.last);
    return out;
  }

  /// ウェイポイント（セル中心）まで連続制御で走行する。
  ///
  /// 各周期で安全ゲート（リンク→ポーズ→スキャン）を通ってからでないと
  /// 駆動指令を出さない。ゲートで止まった場合の結果:
  /// - リンク切断・送信失敗 → [DriveOutcome.linkLost]（地図に書かない）
  /// - センサー喪失から復帰 → [DriveOutcome.replan]
  /// - センサーが復帰しない → [DriveOutcome.sensorLost]
  Future<DriveOutcome> driveToWaypoint(Cell waypoint) async {
    final target = cellCenter(params, waypoint);
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    while (!_aborted && sw.elapsed < waypointTimeout) {
      final linkOut = _checkLink();
      if (linkOut != null) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return linkOut;
      }
      final gp = await _gatePose();
      if (gp != null) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return gp;
      }
      final gs = await _gateScan();
      if (gs != null) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return gs;
      }
      final p = _pose;
      final dx = target.x - p.x;
      final dy = target.y - p.y;
      final dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      if (dist < 0.07) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return DriveOutcome.arrived;
      }
      if (_proximityBlocked) {
        await link.sendJog('s');
        _markBlockedAhead();
        return DriveOutcome.blocked;
      }
      final desired = math.atan2(dy, dx);
      final err = _normAngle(desired - p.heading);
      hudTargetHeading = desired;
      hudTargetDistM = dist;
      final steer = _computePulseSteer(err);
      var drive = dist < 0.18 ? math.min(cruiseDrive, 0.40) : cruiseDrive;
      if (steer.abs() > 0.5) drive = math.min(drive, 0.38);
      if (!await _sendNorm(drive, steer)) {
        // 送信失敗 = リンクが死んでいる（R3）。障害物として扱わない。
        lastStopReason = 'link-lost';
        _status('指令の送信に失敗しました（リンク切断の疑い）。停止します');
        await link.sendJog('s');
        return DriveOutcome.linkLost;
      }
      applyScanIfMoved();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
    }
    await link.sendJog('s');
    if (_aborted) return DriveOutcome.aborted;
    // ゲートを通り続けたのに進めなかった = 実質的に塞がれている（安全側）。
    // _markBlockedAhead 側にも健全性ガードがある。
    _markBlockedAhead();
    return DriveOutcome.timeout;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 探索（中核 Explorer と同じ手順の非同期版）
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<ExploreResult> explore({
    void Function(Float32List snapshot, Cell robot)? onFrame,
    Duration maxDuration = const Duration(minutes: 8),
    bool initialSpin = false,
  }) async {
    if (_busy) throw StateError('既に動作中です');
    _busy = true;
    _aborted = false;
    lastStopReason = null;
    final p = params;
    var steps = 0;
    var scans = 0;
    var failedPlans = 0;
    var sensorReplans = 0;
    var covAtLastReplan = -1.0;
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    try {
      _status('初期スキャン中…');
      final start = currentCell;
      grid.logOdds[grid.index(start.r, start.c)] = p.lFree;
      await captureScan();
      scans++;
      if (initialSpin) {
        await rotationScan();
        scans += 12;
      }

      var cov = grid.coverage;
      var endReason = 'step-limit';

      while (cov < p.targetCoverage &&
          steps < 2000 &&
          !_aborted &&
          sw.elapsed < maxDuration) {
        final robot = currentCell;
        final mask = grid.planMask(forcedFree: robot);
        final fronts = grid.frontierCells(mask);
        if (fronts.isEmpty) {
          endReason = 'no-frontier';
          break;
        }
        fronts.sort((a, b) {
          final da =
              (a.r - robot.r) * (a.r - robot.r) +
              (a.c - robot.c) * (a.c - robot.c);
          final db =
              (b.r - robot.r) * (b.r - robot.r) +
              (b.c - robot.c) * (b.c - robot.c);
          return da.compareTo(db);
        });
        List<Cell>? path;
        for (final goal in fronts.take(60)) {
          if (goal == robot) continue;
          path = findPath(
            gridW: p.gridW,
            gridH: p.gridH,
            isFree: (i) => mask[i] == 1,
            start: robot,
            goal: goal,
          );
          if (path != null) break;
        }

        if (path == null || path.length < 2) {
          failedPlans++;
          if (failedPlans >= 2) {
            endReason = 'no-progress';
            break;
          }
          if (initialSpin) {
            await rotationScan();
            scans += 12;
          } else {
            await rotateTo(heading + math.pi / 3);
            await captureScan();
            scans++;
          }
          if (grid.coverage <= cov) {
            endReason = 'no-progress';
            break;
          }
          cov = grid.coverage;
          continue;
        }
        failedPlans = 0;
        _status('フロンティアへ移動中…（カバー率 ${(cov * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%）');

        final waypoints = _compressWaypoints(path);
        var blockedPath = false;
        String? fatal;
        for (final wp in waypoints.skip(1)) {
          final before = currentCell;
          final outcome = await driveToWaypoint(wp);
          final after = currentCell;
          final dCells = math.max(
            (after.r - before.r).abs(),
            (after.c - before.c).abs(),
          );
          steps += dCells;
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.linkLost) {
            fatal = 'link-lost';
            break;
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.sensorLost) {
            fatal = lastStopReason ?? 'sensor-lost';
            break;
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.replan) {
            // センサー復帰。現在位置から計画し直す（回転スキャン経由）。
            if (cov <= covAtLastReplan + 1e-9) {
              sensorReplans++;
            } else {
              sensorReplans = 1;
            }
            covAtLastReplan = cov;
            break;
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.blocked ||
              outcome == DriveOutcome.timeout) {
            blockedPath = true;
            break;
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.aborted) break;
          await captureScan();
          scans++;
          if (steps % 10 == 0) {
            onFrame?.call(Float32List.fromList(grid.logOdds), currentCell);
          }
        }
        if (fatal != null) {
          endReason = fatal;
          break;
        }
        if (sensorReplans >= 3) {
          endReason = 'sensor-unstable';
          break;
        }
        if (_aborted) {
          endReason = 'aborted';
          break;
        }
        if (!blockedPath) {
          if (initialSpin) {
            await rotationScan();
            scans += 12;
          } else {
            await captureScan();
            scans++;
          }
        }
        cov = grid.coverage;
      }

      if (_aborted) endReason = 'aborted';
      final reached = cov >= p.targetCoverage;
      if (reached) endReason = 'target';
      _status('探索終了: $endReason（カバー率 ${(cov * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%）');
      return ExploreResult(
        steps: steps,
        scans: scans,
        coverage: cov,
        targetReached: reached,
        endReason: endReason,
      );
    } finally {
      hudTargetHeading = null;
      hudTargetDistM = null;
      await link.sendJog('s');
      _busy = false;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // ゴール指定ナビ（中核 GoalNavigator と同じ手順の非同期版）
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<GoToResult> goTo({
    required Cell goal,
    KeepOutZones? keepOut,
    void Function(Float32List snapshot, Cell robot)? onFrame,
  }) async {
    if (_busy) throw StateError('既に動作中です');
    _busy = true;
    _aborted = false;
    lastStopReason = null;
    final p = params;
    final start = currentCell;
    final traversed = <Cell>[start];
    var steps = 0;
    var replans = 0;
    var lengthM = 0.0;

    GoToResult fail(String reason) => GoToResult(
      success: false,
      steps: steps,
      pathLengthM: lengthM,
      replans: replans,
      failureReason: reason,
      traversed: traversed,
    );

    try {
      if (!grid.inBounds(goal.r, goal.c)) return fail('goal-out-of-bounds');
      final goalIdx = grid.index(goal.r, goal.c);
      if (grid.logOdds[goalIdx] == 0) return fail('goal-unknown');
      if (!grid.isPlanFree(goalIdx)) return fail('goal-occupied');
      if (keepOut != null && keepOut.containsIndex(goalIdx)) {
        return fail('goal-keepout');
      }

      var noProgressPlans = 0;
      while (currentCell != goal) {
        if (_aborted) return fail('aborted');
        if (steps >= 2000) return fail('step-limit');
        final current = currentCell;
        final mask = grid.planMask(forcedFree: current);
        if (keepOut != null) {
          final curIdx = grid.index(current.r, current.c);
          for (final i in keepOut.cells) {
            if (i != curIdx) mask[i] = 0;
          }
        }
        final path = findPath(
          gridW: p.gridW,
          gridH: p.gridH,
          isFree: (i) => mask[i] == 1,
          start: current,
          goal: goal,
        );
        if (path == null || path.length < 2) return fail('unreachable');

        final waypoints = _compressWaypoints(path);
        var blocked = false;
        var movedThisPlan = 0;
        Pose? lastPose = poseFeed.latest;
        for (final wp in waypoints.skip(1)) {
          final outcome = await driveToWaypoint(wp);
          final now = poseFeed.latest;
          if (lastPose != null && now != null) {
            final dx = now.x - lastPose.x;
            final dy = now.y - lastPose.y;
            final d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
            lengthM += d;
            steps += (d / p.cellSize).round();
            movedThisPlan += (d / p.cellSize).round();
          }
          lastPose = now;
          final cc = currentCell;
          if (traversed.last != cc) traversed.add(cc);
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.linkLost) return fail('link-lost');
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.sensorLost) {
            return fail(lastStopReason ?? 'sensor-lost');
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.blocked ||
              outcome == DriveOutcome.timeout ||
              outcome == DriveOutcome.replan) {
            // replan はセンサー復帰後の再計画。通常の塞がりと同じ
            // 経路で数えるが、障害物の記録は driveToWaypoint 側で
            // 行われていない（ゲート経由のため）。
            blocked = true;
            break;
          }
          if (outcome == DriveOutcome.aborted) return fail('aborted');
          await captureScan();
          onFrame?.call(Float32List.fromList(grid.logOdds), cc);
          if (cc == goal) break;
        }

        if (currentCell == goal) break;
        if (blocked) {
          replans++;
          if (movedThisPlan == 0) {
            noProgressPlans++;
            if (noProgressPlans >= 2) return fail('blocked-no-progress');
          } else {
            noProgressPlans = 0;
          }
          if (replans > 20) return fail('too-many-replans');
        }
      }

      return GoToResult(
        success: true,
        steps: steps,
        pathLengthM: lengthM,
        replans: replans,
        failureReason: null,
        traversed: traversed,
      );
    } finally {
      hudTargetHeading = null;
      hudTargetDistM = null;
      await link.sendJog('s');
      _busy = false;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // VFH フロアマップ作成（21 本深度レイ＋フロンティア誘導）
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  /// 深度スキャン（21 本）の隣接 3 本移動平均に加え、未探索領域（logOdds == 0）
  /// 利得と最寄りフロンティア方位をスコア化して進路を選出し、パルス当て舵で
  /// 走行しながら占有格子地図を完成させる。
  /// 前方中央コーンが 0.32 m 未満に詰まった場合は自動切り返しで回避する。
  Future<void> cruiseVfh({
    void Function(Float32List snapshot, Cell robot)? onFrame,
    Duration maxDuration = const Duration(minutes: 10),
  }) async {
    if (_busy) throw StateError('既に動作中です');
    _busy = true;
    _aborted = false;
    lastStopReason = null;
    final p = params;
    final offsets = rayOffsets(p);
    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();
    var ticks = 0;
    double? frontierBearing;

    try {
      _status('フロアマップ作成（VFH）を開始…');
      if (_gateBlocks(await _gatePose())) {
        return;
      }
      final start = currentCell;
      grid.logOdds[grid.index(start.r, start.c)] = p.lFree;
      await captureScan();
      onFrame?.call(Float32List.fromList(grid.logOdds), currentCell);

      while (!_aborted &&
          sw.elapsed < maxDuration &&
          grid.coverage < p.targetCoverage) {
        if (_checkLink() != null) break;
        if (_gateBlocks(await _gatePose())) break;
        if (_gateBlocks(await _gateScan())) break;

        final pose = _pose;
        final scan = feed.latest;
        if (scan == null || scan.ranges.isEmpty) {
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
          continue;
        }
        final nRays = math.min(offsets.length, scan.ranges.length);

        double rayDist(int idx) {
          final r = scan.ranges[idx];
          if (!r.isFinite || r <= 0) return 2.2;
          return r.clamp(0.05, p.maxRange);
        }

        // 車幅コリドー（±12cm）内の前方障害物距離（2番目最小値で単発ノイズを除外）
        final corridorHalfFov = p.fovRad / 2.0;
        final corridorVals = <double>[];
        for (var i = 0; i < nRays; i++) {
          final r = scan.ranges[i];
          if (!r.isFinite || r <= 0) continue;
          final theta =
              nRays == 1 ? 0.0 : -corridorHalfFov + (2 * corridorHalfFov) * i / (nRays - 1);
          final lateralM = (r * math.sin(theta)).abs();
          if (lateralM <= 0.12) {
            corridorVals.add(r * math.cos(theta));
          }
        }
        corridorVals.sort();
        final double? centerMin = corridorVals.isEmpty
            ? null
            : (corridorVals.length >= 2 ? corridorVals[1] : corridorVals[0]);

        final frontBlocked =
            _proximityBlocked || (centerMin != null && centerMin <= 0.42);

        if (frontBlocked) {
          hudTargetHeading = null;
          hudTargetDistM = centerMin ?? proximityStopM;
          _status(
            '前方停止（${(centerMin ?? proximityStopM).toStringAsFixed(2)} m）',
          );
          await link.sendJog('s');
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
          if (_aborted) break;
          applyScanIfMoved();
          onFrame?.call(Float32List.fromList(grid.logOdds), currentCell);
          continue;
        }

        // 1 秒ごとに 0.4 m 以上先の最寄りフロンティア方位を更新（既知エリアの往復回避）
        if (ticks % 10 == 0) {
          final robot = currentCell;
          final mask = grid.planMask(forcedFree: robot);
          final fronts = grid.frontierCells(mask);
          Cell? nearest;
          var bestDistSq = double.infinity;
          for (final f in fronts) {
            final fc = cellCenter(p, f);
            final dx = fc.x - pose.x;
            final dy = fc.y - pose.y;
            final d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
            if (d2 >= 0.16 && d2 < bestDistSq) {
              bestDistSq = d2;
              nearest = f;
            }
          }
          if (nearest != null) {
            final fc = cellCenter(p, nearest);
            frontierBearing = math.atan2(fc.y - pose.y, fc.x - pose.x);
          } else {
            frontierBearing = null;
          }
        }

        // 隣接 3 本移動平均＋未探索セル利得＋フロンティア誘導で進路を選ぶ
        var bestIdx = nRays ~/ 2;
        var bestScore = -1.0;
        final halfFov = p.fovRad / 2.0;
        for (var i = 0; i < nRays; i++) {
          var sum = 0.0;
          var count = 0;
          var minWin = double.infinity;
          for (
            var j = math.max(0, i - 1);
            j <= math.min(nRays - 1, i + 1);
            j++
          ) {
            final d = rayDist(j);
            sum += d;
            if (d < minWin) minWin = d;
            count++;
          }
          final avg = sum / count;
          final angleWeight = 1.0 - 0.12 * (offsets[i].abs() / halfFov);
          final measuredBonus = (scan.ranges[i].isFinite && scan.ranges[i] > 0)
              ? 0.30
              : 0.0;

          final double score;
          if (minWin < 0.32) {
            score = minWin * 0.3;
          } else {
            final ang = scan.pose.heading + offsets[i];
            final ca = math.cos(ang);
            final sa = math.sin(ang);
            final probeEnd = math.min(avg, 3.0);
            var probeTotal = 0;
            var probeUnknown = 0;
            for (var d = 0.4; d <= probeEnd + 1e-6; d += 0.4) {
              final c = cellOf(p, scan.pose.x + ca * d, scan.pose.y + sa * d);
              if (!grid.inBounds(c.r, c.c)) break;
              probeTotal++;
              if (grid.logOdds[grid.index(c.r, c.c)] == 0) {
                probeUnknown++;
              }
            }
            final unknownRatio = probeTotal > 0
                ? probeUnknown / probeTotal
                : 0.0;
            final frontierBonus = frontierBearing == null
                ? 0.0
                : 0.4 *
                      math.max(
                        0.0,
                        1.0 -
                            _normAngle(frontierBearing - ang).abs() / p.fovRad,
                      );
            score =
                avg * angleWeight +
                measuredBonus +
                0.6 * unknownRatio +
                frontierBonus;
          }
          if (score > bestScore) {
            bestScore = score;
            bestIdx = i;
          }
        }

        final targetHeading = _normAngle(scan.pose.heading + offsets[bestIdx]);
        final targetDist = rayDist(bestIdx);
        hudTargetHeading = targetHeading;
        hudTargetDistM = targetDist;

        final err = _normAngle(targetHeading - pose.heading);
        final steer = _computePulseSteer(err);
        final frontClear = centerMin ?? targetDist;
        var drive = frontClear < 0.65
            ? math.min(cruiseDrive, 0.40)
            : cruiseDrive;
        if (steer.abs() > 0.5) drive = math.min(drive, 0.38);

        if (!await _sendNorm(drive, steer)) {
          lastStopReason = 'link-lost';
          _status('指令の送信に失敗しました（リンク切断の疑い）。停止します');
          break;
        }

        applyScanIfMoved();
        ticks++;
        if (ticks % 5 == 0) {
          final deg = (offsets[bestIdx] * 180 / math.pi).toStringAsFixed(0);
          _status(
            'フロアマップ作成中（VFH）: 進路 ${deg}° / 前方 ${frontClear.toStringAsFixed(2)} m / '
            '${(grid.coverage * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
          );
          onFrame?.call(Float32List.fromList(grid.logOdds), currentCell);
        }
        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
      }
      if (_aborted) {
        _status('フロアマップ作成を停止しました');
      } else if (lastStopReason == null && grid.coverage >= p.targetCoverage) {
        _status(
          'フロアマップ作成完了（カバー率 ${(grid.coverage * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}%）',
        );
      }
    } finally {
      hudTargetHeading = null;
      hudTargetDistM = null;
      await link.sendJog('s');
      _busy = false;
    }
  }
}
```
